Our experiments indicate that LLM self-evolution is more reliable when it expands the proposal space under an auditable controller, rather than directly choosing experiments.

</details>


### [75] [Zero-shot generalization of transformer neural operators to larger domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14597)
*Armand de Villeroché,Sibo Cheng,Vincent Le Guen,Marc Bocquet,Rem-Sophia Mouradi,Patrick Armand,Alban Farchi,Patrick Massin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在Transformer神经算子中引入可分解的局部性偏置，结合旋转位置编码，实现零样本泛化到更大空间域（域扩展），在多个PDE基准和3D工业气流应用中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的神经算子隐式假设固定域大小，无法泛化到训练时未见的大尺度空间域，限制了零样本推理能力。

Method: 在注意力对数计算中引入可分解的局部性偏置，实现精细可控的局部性，同时保持与查询-键内积的完全可分解性，兼容优化的注意力核；结合旋转位置编码（RoPE）实现具可控空间支持的表达性嵌入，无需改变Transformer架构。

Result: 在两个PDE基准和一个3D工业大气流应用上，该方法在零样本泛化到更大域时显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 域扩展场景本质上要求空间局部性和平移等变性，所提方法通过局部性偏置与RoPE的结合有效解决了这一挑战，在不改变架构的前提下实现了出色的零样本大域泛化。

Abstract: Transformer-based neural operators have shown remarkable performance for approximating solution operators of partial differential equations on complex geometries. However, existing approaches implicitly assume a fixed domain size, which limits their ability to generalize at inference. In this work, we investigate domain extension, namely zero-shot inference on spatial domains that are significantly larger than those encountered during training. We argue that this setting fundamentally requires spatial locality and translation equivariance. We propose to implement this locality via a decomposable bias in the attention logits computation, enabling finely controllable locality while remaining fully decomposable into query-key inner products and directly compatible with optimized attention kernels. Combined with rotary positional embeddings, it enables expressive embeddings with controllable spatial support without altering the transformer architecture. We empirically show that our approach substantially improves zero-shot generalization to larger domains across two PDE benchmarks and a 3D industrial atmospheric flow application. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/cerea-daml/domain-extension.

</details>


### [76] [Realizing Native INT8 Compute for Diffusion Transformers on Consumer GPUs: A Fused INT8 GEMM Kernel for Ideogram 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14598)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对消费级Ampere GPU上扩散Transformer的INT8量化推理速度反不及FP8/NF4的问题，本文发现其根源在于现有"INT8"实现实质上是先量化再反量化回bf16进行矩阵乘法，未能利用GPU的INT8张量核心。作者通过一个融合的Triton INT8 GEMM内核（int8×int8→int32，结合逐token×逐通道反量化和bias融合），替代Ideogram 4.0扩散Transformer线性层中的反量化到bf16路径，在RTX 3090上实现了2.8-4.2x每GEMM加速、端到端约9-10%提速，使INT8从最慢变体变为最快，1024px图像生成变得单卡可行，且质量无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散Transformer的INT8后训练量化在消费级Ampere GPU上实际上比FP8和NF4更慢，原因是软件实现中INT8量化后立即反量化回bf16进行矩阵乘法，从未真正调用GPU的INT8张量核心，导致硬件的计算优势未被利用。

Method: 设计并实现了一个融合的Triton INT8 GEMM内核：在Ampere张量核心上执行int8×int8→int32矩阵乘法，将逐token×逐通道反量化和bias融合到epilogue中，并按GEMM形状自动调优。将此内核替换Ideogram 4.0扩散Transformer线性层中原有的反量化到bf16路径。

Result: 每GEMM相比bf16加速2.8-4.2x；端到端768px图像生成约9-10%加速；1024px图像生成在单张RTX 3090上耗时156.5s，优于NF4（164.5s）和FP8（172.9s）；PickScore/CLIPScore无质量损失；内核在int8×int8→int32累加阶段与torch._int_mm位精确，反量化输出余弦相似度1.0且无NaN。

Conclusion: INT8从最慢变体变为最快，1024px图像生成变为单GPU可行。主要目标（超越FP8约9.5%）达成；超越NF4约4.9%的margin在单次运行的方差内，可视为与拉伸目标一致。但该优化仅限于消费级Ampere GPU，在A100和B200上该内核反而输给其原生bf16/FP8路径。

Abstract: Post-training INT8 (W8A8) quantization of diffusion transformers is widely deployed as a speed optimization, yet on consumer Ampere GPUs it is frequently slower than the FP8 and NF4 alternatives it is meant to beat. We trace this to a software artifact: the production "INT8" forward quantizes weights and activations only to immediately dequantize them back to bf16 and run a bf16 matrix multiply, never engaging the GPU's INT8 tensor cores, so the hardware's compute advantage is left entirely unrealized. We close this gap with a single fused Triton INT8 GEMM (int8xint8->int32 on Ampere tensor cores, with per-token x per-channel dequantization and bias folded into the epilogue, autotuned per GEMM shape) dropped into the Ideogram 4.0 diffusion transformer's linear layers in place of the dequantize-to-bf16 path. In the kernel, the int8xint8->int32 accumulation is bit-exact against torch._int_mm and the dequantized output matches the reference at cosine similarity 1.0 with no NaNs, running 2.8-4.2x faster than bf16 per GEMM. End to end it delivers a ~1.1x (~9-10%) speedup at 768px, and at 1024px it generates an image in 156.5 s on a single RTX 3090, faster than the single-card NF4 (164.5 s) and FP8 (172.9 s) baselines, at no measurable quality cost on these point estimates (PickScore/CLIPScore). INT8 thus goes from the slowest variant to the fastest, and 1024px becomes single-GPU feasible. The primary speed criterion (beat FP8, by ~9.5%) is comfortably met; the NF4 margin (~4.9%, single-run n=4) is within run-to-run variance we did not quantify and is best read as consistent with meeting the stretch target. We close with an honest deployment map: the win is specific to consumer Ampere, and on A100 and B200 the same kernel loses to those cards' fast native bf16/FP8 paths.

</details>


### [77] [A Statistical and Machine Learning Framework for Operational Threshold Detection and Deployable Dispatch Controller Development in Hydrogen Multi-Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14601)
*Shadi Heenatigala,Hasanika Samarasinghe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个统计与机器学习框架，利用一年高分辨率运行数据对氢基多能源系统（H-MES）进行表征。统计发现系统呈二元运行模式（由可再生能源盈余驱动），太阳辐照度解释了氢产量45.7%的秩方差（大效应量）；随机森林分析揭示风速具有高预测重要性（r=0.167弱相关下的非线性关系）；序列模型利用24小时强自相关（r=0.845）进行运行预测，强化学习智能体优化了氢气收益调度。核心贡献在于证明统计与机器学习方法对H-MES建模与控制具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢基多能源系统（H-MES）的运行特性复杂，传统统计方法和机器学习方法各有局限，缺乏一个统一的框架来全面理解和优化其运行。作者希望证明统计方法与机器学习方法在H-MES建模与控制中是互补的，而非相互替代。

Method: 基于一年高分辨率运行数据，综合运用：（1）统计分析方法（秩方差分析、多元回归），（2）随机森林（处理非线性关系），（3）序列模型（利用自相关性进行运行预测），（4）强化学习智能体（优化氢气收益调度）。

Result: 太阳辐照度解释氢产量45.7%的秩方差（大效应量）；电力需求呈弱反向抑制效应（ε²=0.126）；电解槽功率为优势线性预测因子，存在太阳-风协同交互；风速在随机森林中预测重要性排名第一（双变量r=0.167）；24小时自相关系数r=0.845；强化学习成功优化收益调度。

Conclusion: 统计与机器学习方法对H-MES建模与控制具有互补性——统计提供可解释的结构化洞察，机器学习捕捉非线性动态和优化策略，两者协同有助于更全面的系统理解和运行优化。

Abstract: This study presents a statistical and machine learning framework for characterizing a hydrogen-based multi-energy system (H-MES) using one year of high-resolution operational data. Statistical analysis revealed a binary operation driven by renewable surplus, with solar irradiance explaining 45.7% of rank-based variance in hydrogen production, a large effect by conventional standards. Only high-irradiance periods triggered meaningful electrolyzer engagement, while electricity demand exerted a weaker inverse suppression effect ($ε^2 = 0.126$). Multiple regression confirmed electrolyzer power as the dominant linear predictor, with a synergistic solar-wind interaction. Notably, Random Forest analysis ranked wind output first in predictive importance despite its weak bivariate correlation (r = 0.167), revealing non-linear dynamics invisible to parametric methods. A sequence model exploited strong 24-hour autocorrelation (r = 0.845) for operational forecasting, while a reinforcement learning agent optimized hydrogen revenue dispatch. The core contribution is demonstrating that statistical and machine learning approaches are complementary for H-MES modeling and control.

</details>


### [78] [A Comparative Study of Deep Learning Architectures for Multi-Horizon Behavioural Forecasting for Mobile Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14604)
*Pavlos Nicolaou,Kleanthis Malialis,Artemis Kontou,Panayiotis Kolios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了六种深度学习架构、两种零样本基础模型（FM）及统计基线在可穿戴设备行为时间序列预测中的表现，基于三个公开数据集（800+参与者）评估了1-8天预测 horizon、跨群体泛化性、微调策略及模型迁移能力。关键发现：无单一架构绝对占优，PatchTST在训练模型中领先但前三名差异不显著；TimesFM在零样本场景下匹配甚至超越训练模型，尤其在小数据场景；参与者级微调降低RMSE 16-60%，睡眠改善最大、步数最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备产生丰富行为时间序列数据，可用于主动健康干预，但缺乏对不同现代预测架构在这些数据上的系统性比较，尤其是跨群体泛化性、参与者级微调效果及多天预测准确性退化情况尚不明确。

Method: 在三个公开数据集（共800+参与者）上，对六种深度学习架构（包括PatchTST、TCN、MLP、Transformer等）、两种零样本基础模型（TimesFM等）及统计基线进行1-8天多horizon预测基准测试，报告步数、屏幕使用时间和睡眠时长等特征维度的指标，并进行个性化微调研究和FM迁移性评估。

Result: (i) 无单一架构绝对占优，PatchTST在训练模型中领先，但前三名（TCN、MLP、Transformer）性能无显著差异；(ii) TimesFM在零样本场景下匹配或超越训练模型，尤其低数据场景；(iii) 参与者级微调降低RMSE 16-60%，睡眠改善最大、步数改善最小。

Conclusion: 该研究为移动健康预测提供了架构选择、基础模型适用性及个性化策略的实践指导，是首个联合评估现代深度学习、基础模型和个性化方法在可穿戴设备多horizon行为预测中的研究。

Abstract: Wearable devices and smartphones generate rich behavioural time series that can support proactive health interventions, yet systematic comparisons of modern forecasting architectures for these data are lacking. In particular, it remains unclear how models generalise across populations, how different architectures respond to participant-level fine-tuning and how forecasting accuracy degrades across multi-day horizons. We benchmark six deep learning architectures, two zero-shot Foundation Models (FM) and statistical baselines on three public datasets encompassing over 800 participants, reporting per-feature metrics for step counts, screen time and sleep duration across 1-8 day horizons. We further conduct a per-feature personalisation study across all six architectures and assess FM transferability across dataset sizes and temporal granularities. Our key findings are: (i) no single architecture dominates, PatchTST leads among trained models while the three runners-up (TCN, MLP, Transformer) show no meaningful performance difference; (ii) the FM TimesFM matches or exceeds trained models zero-shot, especially in low-data regimes and (iii) participant-level fine-tuning reduces per-feature RMSE by 16-60\%, with sleep benefiting most and step counts least. These results provide practical guidance on architecture selection, FM applicability and personalisation strategies for mobile health forecasting. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to jointly evaluate modern deep learning, FMs and personalisation for multi-horizon behavioural forecasting from wearables.

</details>


### [79] [Expert-Driven Survival Machines: Improving Stratification and Interpretability in Multiple Clinical Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14608)
*Farica Zhuang,Zixuan Wen,Christos Davatzikos,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合专家模型的自适应深度聚类生存分析框架（AdaCSM），通过路由专家机制实现患者动态分配到特化的风险预测器，同时在生存预测和亚型聚类上取得更好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生存模型为所有患者学习单一特征表示，忽略了不同患者亚组间的重要差异，无法捕捉异质性生存模式。

Method: 提出AdaCSM框架，引入基于路由的专家机制，实现条件特化；在参数化生存建模框架内动态分配患者到专门的风险预测器，同时优化生存预测和亚型聚类目标。

Result: 在多个真实世界纵向临床队列（涵盖多种疾病领域）上与现有最优生存模型和深度聚类模型对比，方法在预测性能和可解释性上均取得提升。

Conclusion: 提出的AdaCSM框架通过混合专家机制有效建模异质性生存模式，实现了更优的风险分层和可解释的生存分析结果。

Abstract: Survival prediction plays a central role for healthcare providers and clinical researchers. Accurate risk stratification enables early intervention and improved patient management. Most existing deep survival models learn one common feature representation for all patients, which may hide important differences between patient subgroups. In contrast, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) framework allows different parts of the model to focus on different patient patterns, leading to more individualized representations. Therefore, in this work, we propose a mixture-of-experts enhanced adaptive deep clustering survival framework (AdaCSM) for modeling such heterogeneous survival patterns. We introduce a routing-based expert mechanism that enables conditional specialization within a parametric survival modeling framework. The proposed architecture allocates patients to specialized risk predictors dynamically while preserving the patient survival and subtype clustering objectives. We compare our method with state-of-the-art survival and deep clustering models on multiple real-world longitudinal clinical cohorts spanning diverse disease domains. The proposed method demonstrates improved predictive performance and leads to interpretable results in survival analysis.

</details>


### [80] [Neither Parallel Nor Sequential: How DiffusionGemma Actually Commits Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14620)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性测量DiffusionGemma 26B模型的解码顺序，发现其并非真正的并行或块自回归解码，而是存在从左到右的偏置，且该偏置强度取决于分析粒度；模型以大批量同时提交token，批次内顺序真正未定义而非仅未观测到；行为具有任务依赖性。核心贡献在于提出了测量解码顺序时必须处理的方法论陷阱（尾随EOS填充、组内混杂、提交非单调性、块大小敏感性、大批量平局）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源的扩散语言模型被宣传为并行的非自回归解码器，但实际检查点的token提交顺序几乎从未被实际测量过。现有的认知与真实行为之间存在显著差距。

Method: 对DiffusionGemma 26B（基于Gemma 4的掩码离散扩散混合专家模型）的采样器accept步骤进行hook，记录每个画布位置的提交时间、顺序和置信度。在686个提示、六种场景的探测套件上进行系统性测量。

Result: 1) 解码存在部分从左到右的提交偏置，但token级顺序很弱，随分析粒度粗化而平滑增强；2) "块大小"是测量标尺的人为产物而非架构属性；3) 模型以大批量同时提交，批次内顺序真正未定义；4) 行为具有任务依赖性：结构化JSON以任意顺序提交，数学推理中位置提交置信度与正确性相关，事实回忆则无信号；5) 提交激进，在预算内晚期爆发完成，任务准确率与自回归版Gemma 4相当。

Conclusion: 测量解码顺序必须处理尾随EOS填充、组内混杂、提交非单调性、块大小敏感性和大批量平局这五类方法论陷阱，否则会产生误导性结论。

Abstract: Open diffusion language models are marketed as parallel, non-autoregressive decoders, yet the order in which a shipped checkpoint actually commits its tokens is almost never measured. We instrument DiffusionGemma 26B, a masked discrete-diffusion mixture-of-experts model built on Gemma 4, hooking its sampler's accept step to record which canvas positions commit, when, and at what confidence. Across a 686-prompt, six-regime probe suite we find that its decoding is neither parallel nor block-autoregressive: it follows a partial left-to-right commit bias whose apparent strength depends almost entirely on the granularity at which you look. Order is weak token by token and strengthens smoothly as the analysis is coarsened, so the model's "block size" turns out to be an artifact of the measuring ruler rather than the architecture. The model commits in large simultaneous batches, leaving much of the within-batch order genuinely undefined rather than merely unobserved. The behaviour is regime-dependent: structured JSON is committed in essentially arbitrary order, and a position's commit confidence tracks correctness on mathematical reasoning but carries no signal on factual recall. Commitment is aggressive, finishing in a short late burst well inside the step budget, while task accuracy matches the model's autoregressive Gemma-4 sibling. Beyond these findings, our central contribution is methodological: measuring decoding order honestly demands handling trailing-EOS padding, within-regime confounding, commit non-monotonicity, block-size sensitivity, and large commit-batch ties, each of which can otherwise manufacture a decoding-order result that is not really there.

</details>


### [81] [Graph Diffusion Residuals for Control-Function Instrumental Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14636)
*Rui Wu,Zongyuan Chen,Hong Xie,Defu Lian,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应各向异性仪器热流（A-IHF），一种用于灵活控制函数的确定性图扩散残差提取器。A-IHF将处理变量视为一阶段特征图上的信号，通过导流扩散检测处理变量的跳跃并调整导度，利用稀疏图预解式计算生成的控制函数。其观测选择规则仅使用(Z,X)，结合图广义交叉验证、粗糙度、残差化处理相关性和图可接受性过滤。理论分析将误差分解为结构泄露、残差衰减和残差化处理变异，给出有限样本界、潜在分段光滑几何下的图可接受性速率和有限路径选择校准。在54个合成基准单元中，受保护的观测A-IHF具有最低的平均结构响应MSE；A-IHF系列在32个单元中优于最佳非A-IHF基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制函数工具变量估计器需要一阶段残差而非仅一阶段预测。高容量一阶段模型可能插值处理变量，导致为结果方程留下的残差信息过少。现有方法在处理高维或复杂结构时，一阶段残差提取不足，影响工具变量估计的有效性。

Method: 提出自适应各向异性仪器热流（A-IHF），是一种基于图扩散的确定性残差提取方法。具体包括：将处理变量视为一阶段特征图上的信号；通过导流扩散检测处理变量的大幅跳跃；在跳跃处衰减导度；利用稀疏图预解式计算生成的控制函数。观测选择规则结合图广义交叉验证、粗糙度、残差化处理相关性和图可接受性过滤。

Result: 在54个合成基准单元上，受保护的观测A-IHF具有最低的平均结构响应MSE；A-IHF系列在32个单元中优于最佳非A-IHF基线。当图能够捕获分段光滑的一阶段结构时，性能最强。

Conclusion: A-IHF作为一种新颖的控制函数残差提取方法，在处理复杂一阶段结构时表现出优越性能，尤其在图能有效捕捉分段光滑结构的情况下。该方法为工具变量估计中控制函数的设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Control-function instrumental variable estimators need a first-stage residual, not merely a first-stage prediction. High-capacity first stages can interpolate treatment and leave too little residual information for the outcome equation. We study Adaptive Anisotropic Instrumental Heat Flow (A-IHF), a deterministic graph-diffusion residual extractor for flexible control functions. A-IHF treats treatment as a signal on a graph of first-stage features, uses pilot diffusion to detect large treatment jumps, attenuates conductance across those jumps, and computes the generated control with a sparse graph resolvent. Its observational selection rule uses only $(Z,X)$, combining graph generalized cross-validation, roughness, residualized-treatment relevance, and graph-admissibility filtering. The analysis decomposes error into structural leakage, residual attenuation, and residualized treatment variation, yielding finite-sample bounds, graph-admissibility rates under latent piecewise-smooth geometry, and finite-path selection calibration. Across 54 synthetic benchmark cells with tuned graph, kernel, tree, boosting, series, and neural control-function baselines, guarded observational A-IHF has the lowest average structural-response MSE; the A-IHF family beats the best non-A-IHF baseline in 32 cells. Performance is strongest when the graph captures piecewise-smooth first-stage structure.

</details>


### [82] [Online Convex Optimization with Sublinear Noisy Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14640)
*Simone Di Gregorio,Anupam Gupta,Stefano Leonardi,Matteo Russo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线凸优化中的成对探测（pairwise probes）模型，证明了即使使用次线性且带噪声的探测预算，也能显著改善最坏情况下的遗憾界，并给出了一个紧的（除对数因子外）上界，该界随噪声参数δ平滑退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将两种近期研究方向（专家设定中的次线性最佳专家查询和在线凸优化中每轮可用的成对比较反馈）统一到一个探测框架中，研究有限探测预算是否能提升全反馈OCO的最坏情况遗憾界。

Method: 提出统一的成对探测模型，结合方差缩减效应和连续指数权重的二阶（基于方差）分析，量化探测带来的收益。

Result: 对于带有k个δ-噪声成对探测的OCO，得到遗憾界为O(min{√(dT ln T), dT ln T/(k|1-2δ|)})，该界在T、k和δ上紧（至多对数因子）。

Conclusion: 即使是次线性且带噪声的探测预算也能在理论上提升全反馈OCO的遗憾界；当应用于有限集K的d个专家预测时，得到的界在所有参数（包括d）上完全紧。

Abstract: We study Online Convex Optimization (OCO) over a convex set $K\subseteq \mathbb R^d$, where in each round $t$ the learner selects $x_t\in K$ and then observes a convex loss $f_t:K\to[0,1]$, with the goal of minimizing regret to the best fixed decision in hindsight. We introduce a unified probing model that generalizes two recent lines of work: sublinear best-expert queries in the experts setting, and pairwise (comparison-based) feedback available every round in OCO. In our framework, the learner has a budget of $k\le T$ pairwise probes; on a probed round it may query two points and learn which one has smaller loss.
  Our main result shows that even a sublinear and noisy probe budget can provably improve worst-case regret in the full feedback OCO regime. With $k$ $δ$-noisy pairwise probes, we obtain: $ \text{Reg}_T \le O\left(\min\left\{\sqrt{dT\ln T},\; \frac{dT\ln T}{k|1-2δ|}\right\}\right) $, which is tight (up to logarithmic factors in $T$) across $T$, $k$ and $δ$. Specifically regarding the noise parameter $δ\in [0,1]$, the regret guarantee smoothly degrades as the oracle response approaches a coin flip, i.e., $δ$ is close to $\frac{1}{2}$. When applying the same techniques to a finite $K$ for the prediction with $d$ experts setting, the resulting rates are instead completely tight in all parameters, including $d$.
  Our analysis gives a streamlined treatment of pairwise probing in OCO by quantifying the benefit of probing via a variance reduction effect, combined with a second-order (variance-based) analysis of Continuous Exponential Weights.

</details>


### [83] [Which Directions Matter? Sparse Design for Affine Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14648)
*Pedro Chumpitaz-Flores,My Duong,Juan S. Borrero,Kaixun Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于有限字典和预算约束的原子不确定性集构建方法，通过覆盖目标（包括梯度、对抗扰动或外样本偏移）进行数据驱动的子集选择，证明了目标函数的单调次模性，支持贪心算法并给出(1-1/e)近似保证和匹配的难度下界，同时提供了损失界证书和半径校准规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒机器学习与优化高度依赖不确定性模型的选择，但如何从有限字典中选取合适的子集来构建不确定性集，使其覆盖关键不确定性方向（如梯度、对抗扰动等），缺乏系统的数据驱动方法。

Method: 定义原子不确定性集及其闭式支撑函数；提出基于覆盖目标的数据驱动选择规则（涵盖梯度、对抗扰动、外样本偏移等多类评估方向）；证明目标函数具有单调性和次模性，从而使用贪心算法求解；给出近似保证(1-1/e)和硬度下界；提供损失界证书和外样本控制下的半径校准规则。

Result: 贪心方法达到(1-1/e)近似保证，且该界匹配理论硬度下界；所提证书能有效限制子集选择带来的损失；半径校准规则实现了对外样本的控制。

Conclusion: 基于覆盖目标的数据驱动子集选择方法为鲁棒优化提供了一种理论上可保证、实践上可操作的不确定性集构建方案，兼具近似最优性、可解释性和外样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Robust machine learning and optimization rely on the uncertainty model choice. We investigate which uncertainty directions a model must cover when defined by a finite dictionary and a budget constraint. Selecting a subset forms an atomic uncertainty set with a closed form support function, yielding tractable robust programs for affine objectives. We propose a data driven selection rule based on a coverage objective over evaluation directions, including gradients, adversarial perturbations, or shifts observed on held out data. We prove this objective is monotone and submodular, supporting a greedy method with a $(1-1/e)$ approximation guarantee and a matching hardness barrier. We also provide a certificate bounding the loss from the selected subset and a radius calibration rule with out of sample control.

</details>


### [84] [Graph Structured Combinatorial Semi-Bandit with Nonlinear Reward Associations through Separable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14650)
*Christoph Bauschmann,Setareh Maghsudi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图结构因果奖励建模、解析再生核方法和泰勒函数过程近似的通用自适应策略，用于在互连数据中高效识别最优结构，具备亚线性时间复杂度和线性数据量复杂度，并在合成与真实交通数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模互连数据中的最优结构识别需要大量采样和计算资源，而非线性统计关系的普遍存在进一步增加了复杂度。现有方法在效率和鲁棒性方面存在不足，亟需一种能自适应学习信号依赖关系且具备理论性能保证的通用框架。

Method: 开发了基于图结构的因果奖励建模、解析再生核方法以及泰勒函数过程近似的通用自适应策略，并建立了时间上次线性、数据量上线性的理论性能保证，同时考虑了噪声干扰、模型收敛和求解空间不匹配等不确定性。

Result: 理论分析证明了算法在时间上具有次线性复杂度、在数据量上具有线性复杂度，并对多种不确定性具备鲁棒性；在合成数据集和真实交通数据集上的数值实验验证了实际有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架在极简条件假设下具有广泛适用性，通过多种扩展修改可适配特定场景，为大规模互连数据中的最优结构识别提供了高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: The identification of optimal structures within vast arrays of interconnected data necessitates significant sampling- and computational effort. Learning and leveraging underlying signal dependencies can improve efficiency and predictive capabilities considerably, but the ubiquity of nonlinear statistical relations amplifies the complexity of such undertakings. In this paper, we develop novel generic and adaptive strategies equipped with routines for graph-based causal reward modeling, analytic reproducing kernel methods, and Taylor approximation of functional processes. We establish theoretical performance guarantees sublinear in time and linear in data volume over time. Our analyses cover robustness to a multitude of uncertainties arising from noise interference, gradual model convergence, and solution space mismatch. The framework's general appeal is substantiated by a minimalistic set of conditions or reliance on prior estimates, while various outlined modifications address specific or extended settings. To demonstrate practical effectiveness, we conduct numerical experiments using both benchmarked synthetic and real-world transportation datasets.

</details>


### [85] [Beyond task performance: Decoding bioacoustic embeddings with speech features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14662)
*Ines Nolasco,Jules Cauzinille,Marius Miron,Gagan Narula,Milad Alizadeh,Emmanuel Fernandez,Matthieu Geist,Ellen Gilsenan-McMahon,Olivier Pietquin,Emmanuel Chemla,Sara Keen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过88个eGeMAPS声学特征和六种分类群的线性/非线性探针，系统揭示了预训练音频嵌入在生物声学中编码的类语音特征，发现无单一模型能覆盖全部特征空间，拼接嵌入性能最佳（响度R²=0.76，基频最难恢复R²=0.33），并基于特征可恢复性与物种级特征显著性的交叉分析提供了数据驱动的模型选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练音频嵌入在生物声学中被广泛使用，但学界对其编码了哪些声学特征、哪些特征对特定任务有用知之甚少，这阻碍了模型透明度并限制了向稀有物种或数据稀缺领域的扩展。

Method: 使用88维eGeMAPS声学特征集，覆盖六个分类群，分别采用线性回归和非线性回归探针，定量评估每个生物声学模型对各类声学属性的编码能力；并通过交叉引用特征可恢复性（R²）与每物种特征显著性（归一化互信息NMI）来推导模型选择依据。

Result: 确认存在"没有免费午餐"模式，即没有单一模型能覆盖完整特征空间；拼接多个模型嵌入可达到最高性能，表明模型间在声学空间覆盖上具有互补性；响度特征编码最好（R²=0.76），基频F0最难恢复（R²=0.33）。

Conclusion: 通过将特征的可恢复性与各物种的特征显著性进行交叉比对，可为生物声学任务提供数据驱动的模型选择指导。

Abstract: Pretrained audio embeddings are standard in bioacoustics, yet little is known about which acoustic features these models encode, nor which are useful for a given task. This hinders transparency and limits extension to rare species or data-scarce domains. Here we reveal which speech-like features are encoded in bioacoustic representations. Using the 88~eGeMAPS features across six taxonomic groups, we apply linear and nonlinear regression probes to quantify which acoustic properties each model captures. Results confirm a ``no free lunch'' pattern: no single model captures the full feature space. A concatenated embedding achieves the highest performance, suggesting complementary acoustic space coverage across models. Loudness features are best encoded ($R^2 = 0.76$) while F0 is hardest to recover ($R^2 = 0.33$). By cross-referencing recoverability with per-species feature salience (NMI), we derive data-driven model selection guidance for bioacoustics.

</details>


### [86] [When to Write and When to Suppress: Route-Specialized Dual Adapters for Memory-Assisted Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14668)
*Yining Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种路由专用双适配器知识编辑方法（\method{}），通过相关性路由器判断是否激活编辑记忆，分别使用编辑适配器修改对象偏好、局部性适配器保持非直接相关提示的原始偏好，在三个基准测试上取得了最优概率偏好准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识编辑系统需要更新选定事实同时保持邻近但无关行为不变，核心设计问题不仅是如何写入编辑，还包括何时抑制它。

Method: 提出\method{}路由专用双适配器编辑器：相关性路由器先判断提示词是否应接收编辑记忆，被路由的提示使用编辑适配器训练为偏好新对象，未路由的非直接提示使用单独的局部性适配器训练为保留或恢复原始对象偏好。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，\method{}在CF、ZSRE和MQuaKE三个基准上分别取得0.8180、0.8946和0.9922的最佳概率偏好准确率；在Qwen3-8B上趋势一致。路由器消融实验显示相关记忆边界因数据集而异。组件和模块消融显示增益主要来自编辑注入与离路抑制的分离。

Conclusion: \method{}通过路由机制区分何时应用编辑、何时保持局部性，有效解决了知识编辑中编辑与局部性保持的权衡问题。

Abstract: Knowledge editing systems must update selected facts while preserving nearby but irrelevant behavior. This paper studies this problem in a memory-assisted setting where an edit memory is retrieved at inference time and a parameter-efficient adapter corrects the model's object preference. We argue that the central design question is not only how to write an edit, but also when to suppress it. We introduce \method{}, a route-specialized dual-adapter editor. A relevance router first decides whether a prompt should receive an edit memory. Routed prompts use an edit adapter trained to prefer the new object over the original object; unrouted non-direct prompts use a separate locality adapter trained to preserve or restore the original-object preference. We evaluate \method{} on three 1,000-case protocols, \cf{}, \zsre{}, and \mquake{}, under the same memory protocol and two 7B/8B base models. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, \method{} obtains the best overall probability-preference accuracy on all three benchmarks: 0.8180 on \cf{}, 0.8946 on \zsre{}, and 0.9922 on \mquake{}. The same trend holds on Qwen3-8B. Router ablations show that the relevant memory boundary differs across datasets: a lexical neural router is safest on \cf{}, while BGE embedding routing is better on \zsre{} and \mquake{}. Component and module ablations show that the gain mainly comes from separating edit injection from off-route suppression rather than from simply increasing LoRA capacity.

</details>


### [87] [Compressed Computation is (probably) not Computation in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14673)
*Jai Bhagat,Sara Molas-Medina,Giorgi Giglemiani,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明压缩计算（CC）玩具模型并非超定位计算（computation in superposition）的实例，其性能提升源于输入在噪声残差流中的意外混合，而非真正的超定位计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证CC玩具模型（Braun et al., 2025）是否真正实现了超定位计算，即用50个神经元计算100个ReLU函数的能力是否源于计算超定位机制。

Method: 将训练目标分解为ReLU项和混合项；分析混合矩阵的特征值分布与神经元方向的关系；构建基于混合矩阵的半非负矩阵分解（SNMF）基线进行对比。

Result: 性能增益与混合矩阵大小正相关，移除混合矩阵后增益消失；学习到的神经元方向集中于混合矩阵前50个特征值对应的子空间；SNMF基线虽未完全匹配训练模型，但复现了定性损失曲线并优于先前基线。

Conclusion: CC模型不是超定位计算的合适玩具模型，其表现由输入混合效应主导而非计算超定位机制。

Abstract: We study whether the Compressed Computation (CC) toy model (Braun et al., 2025) is an instance of computation in superposition. The CC model appears to compute 100 ReLU functions with just 50 neurons, achieving a better loss than expected from only representing 50 ReLU functions. We show that the model mixes inputs via its noisy residual stream, corresponding to an unintended mixing matrix in the labels. Splitting the training objective into the ReLU term and the mixing term, we find that performance gains scale with the magnitude of the mixing matrix and vanish when the matrix is removed. The learned neuron directions concentrate in the subspace associated with the top 50 eigenvalues of the mixing matrix, suggesting that the mixing term governs the solution. Finally, a semi-non-negative matrix factorization (SNMF) baseline derived solely from the mixing matrix reproduces the qualitative loss profile and improves on prior baselines, though it does not match the trained model. These results suggest CC is not a suitable toy model of computation in superposition.

</details>


### [88] [Optimal Hidden-Target Learning for Online Inventory Optimization on General Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14679)
*Anthony Pineci,Yunzong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在线库存优化问题中，维护隐藏目标并结合投影执行的原则是最优的，利用范数对齐原理将问题简化为标量队列控制，显著改进了遗憾保证，并提供了匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线库存优化中，库存结转导致可行动作集依赖于历史，传统方法在处理一般凸容量集时遗憾保证不佳，亟需更优的理论框架。

Method: 提出范数对齐原则，将隐藏目标到可行集的距离作为状态变量（与投影使用相同范数），将多维问题降维为一维标量队列控制；以在线梯度下降为基础学习器，结合投影操作。

Result: 在一般凸可行集上，将遗憾从共同需求概率的逆依赖改进为逆平方根依赖，并证明匹配下界；对强凸损失给出首个多项式对数遗憾保证；对一般凸容量集给出动态遗憾保证。

Conclusion: 维护隐藏目标并执行投影的简单原则对任意有界凸容量集上的在线库存优化是最优的，范数对齐为处理状态依赖性提供了通用分析框架。

Abstract: Online inventory optimization (OIO) is online convex optimization with physical memory: inventory carryover makes the feasible action set depend on the past. A natural principle, used in stochastic inventory learning and recently in OIO under a single linear capacity constraint, is to maintain a hidden target chosen by an online learner and implement its projection onto the currently feasible order-up-to set. We prove that this simple principle is optimal for OIO on arbitrary bounded convex capacity sets. With online gradient descent as the base learner, the method improves the best known regret guarantee for OIO on general convex sets from inverse to inverse-square-root dependence on the common-demand probability, and we prove a matching lower bound. The same principle gives the first polylogarithmic regret guarantee for strongly convex losses and the first dynamic regret guarantee adapting to Euclidean path variation on general convex capacity sets.
  The analysis introduces a norm alignment principle: the right state variable is the distance from the hidden target to the feasible set, measured in the same norm as the projection. Under norm alignment, this distance evolves pathwise as a scalar queue, with target movement as arrival and common demand as service. This reduction to one-dimensional queue control resolves the state dependence and extends the guarantees to general convex capacity sets, beyond the reach of prior productwise approaches. Experiments on synthetic and real-world inventory data corroborate the theory.

</details>


### [89] [Flood and Harvest: The Provable Necessity of Trivia for Generating Valuable Mathematics via the Lens of Language Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14688)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Dai Shi,Zheng Gao,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在证明助手辅助下，通过形式语言F与未知有价值语言H之间的嵌套语言生成问题，证明了验证器（证明检查器）无法替代“品味”：它虽能保证声音覆盖（避免假阳性），但无法避免无限量但渐近可忽略的平凡语句生成，并揭示了有限与无限平凡语句允许阈值下的最优覆盖率跃迁（从α/2到1-α/2）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统结合证明助手能够大规模生成形式化数学，但验证器能检查的内容与数学家认为有价值的内容之间存在根本性差距（binding constraint）。本文旨在从信息论和计算学习理论角度，严格建模并刻画这种差距的本质。

Method: 将“生成有价值数学”建模为极限下的嵌套语言生成问题：形式语言F（通过成员查询oracle访问）中包含未知有价值语言H，其核心C已知密度α；输出分为有价值（∈H）、平凡（∈F\H）、幻觉（∉F）。采用Angluin条件进行纤维化刻画，并在压缩模型下实例化。

Result: (1) 验证器无法提供品味：可广度生成的集合与无oracle模型相同；(2) 验证器提供声音覆盖（正确性保证），无验证器则不可能；(3) 关键二分法：有限平凡语句允许下最优覆盖率为α/2，无限平凡语句允许（即使频率趋于0）跃升至1-α/2，且紧界成立；(4) 两个机制均在数学压缩模型中实例化。

Conclusion: 完美的验证器不能替代品味——无限但渐近可忽略的正确但无价值语句流不是工程事故，而是可证明的必然性，因为覆盖未记录的有价值数学必须付出此代价。

Abstract: AI systems coupled to proof assistants now generate formal mathematics at scale, and the gap between what a checker can verify and what a mathematician would value has become the binding constraint. We model the generation of valuable mathematics as nested language generation in the limit: a verifiable formal language $F$, accessed through a membership oracle (the proof checker), contains an unknown valuable language $H \in \mathcal{H}$ revealed only through an adversarial enumeration of a core $C \subseteq H$ of exact density $α$ (the literature). Every output is valuable ($\in H$), trivial ($\in F \setminus H$), or a hallucination ($\notin F$). We settle four questions. First, the verifier is not taste: the collections admitting generation with breadth are exactly those of the oracle-free model, characterized fiber-wise by Angluin's condition. Second, the verifier does buy sound coverage, covering all unseen valuable statements while asserting only valid ones: possible with it, impossible without it; it relocates unavoidable errors from false to trivial. Third, and centrally, a sharp dichotomy on the tight family: generators emitting finitely many trivia achieve optimal coverage $α/2$, while any infinite trivia allowance, even at vanishing rate, jumps the optimum to $1-α/2$ (both tight, for cores presented as the candidate intersection), and one generator attains both ends. The transition is in trivia count, not rate; the gap $1-α$ is the unrecorded mass. Fourth, both regimes instantiate in a compression model of mathematics. A perfect verifier cannot substitute for taste: the unbounded stream of correct-but-worthless statements is not an engineering accident but a provable necessity, since covering unrecorded valuable mathematics requires an infinite, but asymptotically negligible, stream of certified trivia.

</details>


### [90] [A Complexity Measure for Active Learning in Multi-group Mean Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14690)
*Abdellah Aznag,Rachel Cummings,Adam N. Elmachtoub*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多组均值估计d臂赌博机中的最大风险主动学习目标，建立了局部极小极大框架，提出了首个一般性下界，该下界将难度分解为预算项、异方差指数和方差局部曲率（VLC）三个正交因子。对于光滑类，VLC是方差-费希尔信息的重参数化，具有常见族的闭式解。与最强上界对比显示在广泛范围内接近最优（对数因子内），并在高度异质实例中指出了系统性差距。证明引入了损失诱导的ℓ₁决策空间几何和基于表示的问题生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动学习或多组均值估计方法通常关注平均性能或简单方差假设，缺乏针对最坏情况不确定性（max-risk）的系统性理论下界。本文旨在建立该目标下的信息论下界，揭示问题复杂度的本质结构。

Method: 建立了局部极小极大分析框架，将下界分解为预算、异方差性和VLC三个正交因子；引入了损失诱导的ℓ₁几何结构和基于表示的实例生成器，将困难实例构造转化为随机矩阵计算。

Result: 给出了首个针对max-risk目标的一般性下界，证明了VLC作为模型相关复杂度度量的核心作用；与最优上界对比显示近最优性，并识别了异质场景下的系统性差距。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了多组均值估计中max-risk主动学习问题的本质信息论边界，为异方差场景下的算法设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study a \emph{max-risk} objective for active learning in a multi-group mean estimation $d$-armed bandits: a learner adaptively allocates a budget of $T$ samples across $d$ groups to minimize the worst-case uncertainty index $\max_{k\in[d]}σ_k^2/n_k$, where $σ_k$ is the standard deviation of the distribution of arm $d$, and $n_k$ is the number of times arm $d$ is sampled. We develop a local minimax framework and prove the first general lower bound for this objective, valid for any finite-variance hypothesis class. The bound separates difficulty into three orthogonal factors: a \emph{budget} term, a \emph{heteroscedasticity} index measuring how unevenly the uncertainty is spread across arms, and a model-dependent complexity measure, the \emph{Variance Local Curvature} ($\mathrm{VLC}$), which captures how much information a local change of variance creates inside the hypothesis class. For smooth classes, the $\mathrm{VLC}$ is a reparametrization of a variance--Fisher information, with closed-form values for common families. Benchmarking against the strongest available upper bound shows near-optimality up to logarithmic factors in broad regimes, and pinpoints a systematic gap in highly heterogeneous instances. Our proof introduces two key ingredients: a loss-induced $\ell_1$ geometry on the decision space, and a representation-based instance generator that reduces hard-instance construction to an explicit random matrix calculation.

</details>


### [91] [Persona-Pruner: Sculpting Lightweight Models for Role-Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14695)
*Jinsu Kim,Jihoon Tack,Noah Lee,Jongheon Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对角色扮演场景中为每个角色维护完整大语言模型带来的计算开销问题，本文提出Persona-Pruner框架，通过从单一描述中提取角色专属子网络，实现轻量级角色扮演模型，在极大降低计算成本的同时保持角色扮演性能，性能下降较最强基线减少高达93.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在角色扮演任务中虽然表现出色，但为每个角色（如游戏中的多个NPC）部署一个完整的大模型会导致巨大的计算开销，效率低下。作者质疑是否有必要为单一角色分配完整的大模型，假设特定角色身份仅依赖模型总容量的一部分。

Method: 提出Persona-Pruner框架，通过从单一角色描述中识别并隔离出角色专属的子网络（sub-networks），修剪掉与角色无关的冗余知识，同时保留关键角色特征，从而构建轻量化的角色扮演模型。

Result: 在RoleBench上以LLM-as-a-judge评分评估，Persona-Pruner较最强基线将性能下降减少93.8%，显著优于现有最先进的LLM剪枝技术，同时保持通用语言模型能力。

Conclusion: 证明了可以通过剪枝方式为每个角色生成轻量级专属模型，在不显著牺牲角色扮演质量的前提下大幅降低计算成本，为大规模NPC生态系统等实际应用提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Language Models (LMs) have shown remarkable potential as role-playing chatbots, delivering consistent, stylized interactions when given a specification of a character or user persona. However, applying these capabilities to real-world applications (e.g., ecosystems with numerous NPCs interacting simultaneously) exposes a critical inefficiency due to the excessive computational cost. In this paper, we question the necessity of dedicating a full, generalist model to a single persona, hypothesizing that a specific character identity relies on only a fraction of the model's total capacity. We observe that naively pruning LMs often severely degrades the role-playing performance for a specific persona; it does not distinguish between redundant knowledge and essential character traits. We propose Persona-Pruner, a framework that sculpts a lightweight role-playing model by isolating persona-specific sub-networks from a single description. Our experiments consistently show that Persona-Pruner preserves role-playing performance substantially more effectively than existing state-of-the-art LLM pruning techniques, reducing the performance drop from the dense model by up to 93.8% over the strongest baseline on RoleBench in LLM-as-a-judge score, while still maintaining general LLM capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/jsu-kim/Persona-Pruner.

</details>

### [92] [Non-Markovian Memory-Induced Effects in Quantum Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13716)
*Aarav Shah,Paulo Moniz,Oem Trivedi,Meet J. Vyas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过引入因果记忆核将半经典Wheeler-DeWitt框架扩展到非局域形式，发现分数阶时间演化可作为非局域动力学的有效描述。应用于德西特空间宇宙学涨落，得到原初功率谱的$k^{3/4}$修正，主要影响CMB高l各向异性，并可能导致非高斯性的尺度依赖修正。提出霍金-哈特尔无边界提议的循环扩展可使记忆强度在宇宙历史中演化，为分数阶量子宇宙学提供了基于记忆效应的具体实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准半经典Wheeler-DeWitt框架仅包含局域动力学，无法捕捉量子引力中可能存在的非局域记忆效应。作者希望超越局域近似，在亚领头阶引入因果记忆核来研究宇宙学中的非局域量子引力效应。

Method: 在半经典Wheeler-DeWitt框架的亚领头阶引入因果记忆核，而非直接假设分数阶导数；将分数阶时间演化视为非局域动力学的有效描述；将框架应用于德西特空间宇宙学涨落。

Result: 原初功率谱出现特征性的$k^{3/4}$修正，主要影响CMB高l温度各向异性（与标准半经典量子引力修正相反）；非高斯性方面，双谱产生尺度依赖修正，可能偏离标准压缩极限一致性关系；记忆系数控制短尺度功率，需微调以获得可接受的天体物理环境。

Conclusion: 该框架为分数阶量子宇宙学提供了基于记忆效应的具体实现，指出了非局域量子引力动力学可能的观测特征。霍金-哈特尔无边界提议的循环扩展可能使有效记忆强度在宇宙历史中演化。

Abstract: We study memory effects in quantum cosmology by extending the semiclassical Wheeler-DeWitt framework beyond its usual local form. The main idea is to introduce a causal memory kernel at sub leading order, rather than imposing fractional derivatives directly by hand. In this setting, fractional time evolution appears as an effective description of the underlying nonlocal dynamics. We apply the framework to cosmological perturbations in de Sitter space and find a correction to the primordial power spectrum with a characteristic $k^{3/4}$ scaling. This contribution mainly affects high $l$ CMB temperature anisotropies, in contrast with standard semiclassical quantum gravitational corrections, which are strongest at large angular scales. We also discuss how the same memory-dependent dynamics may affect primordial non-Gaussianity, producing scale dependent corrections to the bispectrum and possible deviations from the usual squeezed limit consistency relation. Since the memory coefficient controls short scale power, it may also influence structure formation and could require some tuning in order to give phenomenologically acceptable astrophysical environments. Finally, we suggest that a cyclic extension of the Hawking-Hartle no-boundary proposal may provide a setting in which the effective memory strength can evolve across successive cosmological histories. In this way, the framework gives a concrete realization of fractional quantum cosmology based on memory effects and also points to possible observational signatures of nonlocal quantum gravitational dynamics.

</details>


### [93] [Decoding the Gravitational Geometry and Stellar Photometric Profiles of Galaxy NGC 7331](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13718)
*Aritra Sanyal Bikramarka S. Choudhury Farook Rahaman*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在广义相对论框架下，利用NGC 7331星系的旋转曲线和WISE W1波段测光数据，结合静态球对称时空和各向异性物质假设，拟合运动学数据并重建度规函数，推导出包络质量、能量密度和切向压强分布。结果显示恒星质量在中大半径处不足以解释总引力质量，表明暗物质主导。通过能量条件、因果约束和圆轨道稳定性验证了模型的物理可行性，并与NFW暗物质轮廓进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的星系旋转曲线分析多基于牛顿引力或修正引力理论，而本研究希望在广义相对论框架下自洽地处理星系质量分布问题，检验相对论效应对暗物质推断的影响，并验证相对论模型的物理可行性。

Method: 采用静态球对称时空假设，引入径向压力为零的各向异性物质模型，利用修改的指数方位角速度律拟合NGC 7331的观测旋转曲线和WISE W1波段光度数据，重建度规函数，进而求解包络质量、能量密度和切向压强分布，并与NFW暗物质轮廓进行对比。

Result: 在中大半径处，恒星质量贡献显著低于总引力质量，表明暗物质主导；构建的相对论模型满足能量条件、因果约束和圆轨道稳定性，物理上自洽；与NFW暗物质轮廓的比较显示两者在描述暗物质分布上具有一致性。

Conclusion: 将旋转曲线数据与光度恒星质量估计在广义相对论框架下相结合，能够为螺旋星系（如NGC 7331）的质量分布提供一致且物理可行的描述，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Galaxy rotation curves provide a powerful probe of mass distributions in spiral galaxies. We present a general relativistic analysis of NGC 7331 using its observed rotation curve and WISE W1 (3.4 $μ$m) photometry to constrain the stellar mass profile. Adopting a static, spherically symmetric spacetime with anisotropic matter (vanishing radial pressure), we fit a modified exponential azimuthal velocity law to kinematic data, reconstructing metric functions and deriving enclosed mass, energy density, and tangential pressure profiles. The stellar mass underpredicts the total gravitating mass at intermediate-to-large radii, indicating dominant dark matter. The physical viability of the resulting relativistic model is examined through energy conditions, causality constraints, and the stability of circular orbits. A comparison with the standard Navarro--Frenk--White dark matter profile has been done. Overall, this study demonstrates that combining rotation-curve data with photometric stellar mass estimates within a general relativistic framework provides a consistent and physically viable description of the mass distribution in spiral galaxies such as NGC~7331.

</details>


### [94] [High-Precision Relativistic Time Scales for Mars Surface and Orbital Clocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13726)
*Slava G. Turyshev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于IAU BCRS/TCB形式论，构建了以火星为中心的后牛顿框架，用于关联质心坐标时、火星坐标时、火星表面时以及轨道/着陆钟的固有时。引入火星坐标时(TCA)，并给出了各时钟变换的长期率与零均值周期项，保留分数频率振幅>5e-18或单向累积计时振幅>0.1 ps的项。数值实现采用GMM-3火星重力场(阶次120)、日火卫一火卫二质点潮，评估了多种轨道场景。300 km近极轨道钟比火星表面时慢4.56微秒/天，火卫一/火卫二距离轨道钟分别快9.13/9.52微秒/天。火星J2定时线在300 km高度约87 ps。本文提供的是参考系与模型保留框架而非最终运行历表，实现亚皮秒系统还需行星历表、火星定向、季节重力、定轨、链路延迟及协方差分析等，特别是季节性CO2交换引起的低阶重力变化是亚皮秒表面时实现的先决条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着火星探测任务对高精度时间和定位的需求日益增长，现有时间基准框架缺乏针对火星环境的详细后牛顿相对论处理。本文旨在建立一套完整的火星中心后牛顿参考系框架，将质心坐标时、火星坐标时、火星表面时以及各类轨道/着陆器的固有时系统性地关联起来，为未来火星导航、引力测量和基础物理实验提供理论依据。

Method: 基于IAU BCRS/TCB形式论进行火星中心扩展，引入Areocentric Coordinate Time (TCA)。采用后牛顿近似保留到c⁻²阶，并通过频率振幅(>5e-18)和累积定时振幅(>0.1 ps)阈值筛选保留项。数值实现使用GMM-3火星重力场(120阶次)、日月火星引力及火卫一火卫二质点潮(去除原点与偶极项)。评估了低轨、火卫一/火卫二距离、静止轨道、大椭圆中继等多种轨道场景。

Result: 给出了各场景下火星钟相对于火星表面时的长期率差：300 km近极轨道慢4.56微秒/天，静止轨道快9.13微秒/天，火卫二距离轨道快9.52微秒/天。火星J2定时线在300 km约87 ps，静止轨道附近仍保持若干ps。近日点缩放太阳潮在大椭圆中继轨道中退化为亚ps项。明确了实现亚ps火星时系统所需的多项额外条件，特别是季节性CO₂交换造成的低阶重力时变问题。

Conclusion: 本文建立的并非最终运行历表，而是一个参考系与模型保留框架。实际实现亚皮秒级火星时间系统需要：选定行星历表、火星定向模型、季节重力模型、航天器精密定轨、校准链路延迟和协方差分析。季节性的CO₂交换引起的低阶重力场时变是亚皮秒火星表面时实现中最关键的未被充分约束的项，必须被建模、监测或经验约束。

Abstract: We develop a Mars-centered post-Newtonian framework for relating barycentric coordinate time, Mars-centered coordinate time, a conventional Mars surface time scale, and the proper times of landed and orbiting clocks. The construction follows the International Astronomical Union BCRS/TCB formalism, introduces Areocentric Coordinate Time (TCA), and writes each clock transformation as a secular rate plus zero-mean periodic terms. Terms are retained when their fractional-frequency amplitude exceeds 5e-18 or their one-way accumulated timing amplitude exceeds 0.1 ps. The numerical realization uses the GMM-3 Mars gravity field through degree and order 120, point-mass tides from the Sun, Phobos, and Deimos with origin and dipole terms removed, and bounds on omitted local c^-4 and external-perturber terms. Representative low-Mars-orbit, areostationary, Phobos-/Deimos-distance, and highly elliptical relay regimes are evaluated. Relative to the adopted Mars surface scale, a 300 km near-polar clock is slower by 4.56 microseconds per day, while areostationary and Deimos-distance clocks are faster by 9.13 and 9.52 microseconds per day. The leading Mars-J2 timing line is about 87 ps at 300 km altitude and remains several ps near areostationary radius for inclined or librating areosynchronous cases; perihelion-scaled solar tides become retained sub-ps terms in high relay orbits. The result is a reference-system and model-retention framework, not a final operational Mars Time Ephemeris. A realized sub-ps system still requires a selected planetary ephemeris, Mars orientation and seasonal-gravity model, spacecraft orbit determination, calibrated link delays, and covariance analysis. Time-variable low-degree gravity from seasonal CO2 exchange is a leading surface-realization term and must be modeled, monitored, or empirically bounded before sub-ps Mars surface-scale claims are made.

</details>


### [95] [\textsc{SPLIT}: a robust semi-coherent inference pipeline for long-inspiral gravitational-wave sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13728)
*Shubham Kejriwal*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种半相干贝叶斯推理框架（SPLIT），用于LISA引力波探测中极端/中等质量比旋近信号的参数估计。通过将数据分段独立分析再非相干合并，该方法有效抑制了长时间相干分析中因波形模型不精确导致的系统性偏差。实验表明，在复杂环境注入下，半相干方法将参数偏差从全相干分析的~4.8σ降至≤0.5σ，尽管信噪比略有损失，但参数精度显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA将探测到持续数月至数年的EMRI/IMRI引力波信号，现有全相位相干分析管道对波形精度要求极高，长时间建模误差会导致严重的系统性参数偏差。为解决这一问题，需要开发对波形模型误差更鲁棒的推断方法。

Method: 提出半相干贝叶斯推理框架，将长时域数据分割为独立块，每块内进行相干分析（精确追踪相位），块间结果非相干合并（仅结合似然而不跨块追踪相位）。开发了GPU加速的Python包SPLIT实现该方法。

Result: 在含环境噪声的注入测试中，全相干真空GR分析产生约4.8σ的最大1维参数偏差，而半相干方法将偏差限制在≤0.5σ以内。尽管信噪比有部分损失，但参数精度获得大幅改善。

Conclusion: 半相干方法在波形模型不完美时，通过牺牲少量信噪比换取对系统性偏差的显著抑制，为LISA及未来引力波探测器的长时域信号分析提供了一种高性价比的折中方案。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will detect gravitational waves (GWs) from dozens of extreme- and intermediate-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs/IMRIs). These sources will stay in-band for months to years, offering extraordinary scientific potential. However, their fully phase-coherent analysis standard in current pipelines imposes stringent waveform accuracy requirements; failing to model the signal over such long durations can result in significant systematic biases. To address this, we formulate a robust semi-coherent Bayesian inference framework that segments the data into independent blocks, analyzes each block coherently, and recombines the results incoherently. By restricting phase-tracking to much shorter block durations, this approach prevents significant accumulation of phase errors. We implement this methodology in \textsc{SPLIT} (Semi-coherent Posteriors for Long-Inspiral Templates), a GPU-accelerated Python package. Applying \textsc{SPLIT} to an environment-rich injection, we demonstrate that while a fully-coherent vacuum-GR analysis incurs a maximum 1D systematic bias of $\approx 4.8σ$ from the truth, the shorter integration window of our semi-coherent approach restricts such biases to $\lesssim 0.5σ$. Overall, despite a fractional loss of optimal signal-to-noise ratio, the substantial improvement in parameter accuracy offered by the semi-coherent approach presents a highly advantageous trade-off for LISA and other future GW detectors.

</details>


### [96] [Constraints on regular black holes with nonminimally coupled electromagnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13773)
*Ana Bokulić,Tajron Jurić,Luka Kanai Pejić,Filip Požar,Ivica Smolić*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了包含非最小耦合相互作用项（RFF、R_ab F^a_c F^bc、R_abcd F^ab F^cd）的引力理论，证明除精细调节耦合常数的情况外，磁荷规则黑洞被排除，类似结论也适用于电荷规则黑洞，并推广到f(R,F⋆F)形式的拉格朗日项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有规则黑洞解的物理现实理论是引力物理学的重要开放问题。过去二十年主要研究最小耦合电磁场理论，而非最小耦合理论尚待探索。

Method: 通过理论分析研究包含RFF、R_ab F^a_c F^bc、R_abcd F^ab F^cd等非最小耦合相互作用项的理论，证明磁荷规则黑洞被排除（除精细调节耦合常数外），并论证类似结论适用于电荷规则黑洞，进一步推广到f(R,F⋆F)形式的拉格朗日项。

Result: 证明磁荷规则黑洞被排除（除精细调节耦合常数外），类似结论适用于电荷规则黑洞，并推广到f(R,F⋆F)形式的拉格朗日项。

Conclusion: 包含非最小耦合相互作用项的引力理论中，规则黑洞解受到严格限制，仅在耦合常数精细调节的极端情况下可能存在。

Abstract: Construction of physically realistic theories admitting regular black hole solutions remains an important open problem in gravitational physics. While theories with electromagnetic fields minimally coupled to gravity have been extensively studied over the past two decades, theories with nonminimal couplings remain comparatively unexplored. We investigate theories containing the interaction terms $R F_{ab}F^{ab}$, $R_{ab} F^a_{\ \, c} \, F^{bc}$ and $R_{abcd} F^{ab} F^{cd}$, which generically arise in low-energy effective Lagrangians. We prove that magnetically charged regular black holes are excluded, except possibly for finely tuned choices of coupling constants, and argue that a similar conclusion applies to electrically charged regular black holes. We further show that similar conclusions hold for Lagrangian terms of the form $f(R,F_{ab}{\star F}^{ab})$.

</details>


### [97] [Relativistic and Newtonian Proca Stars: A Tale of Two Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13785)
*Alberto Diez-Tejedor,Carlos Herdeiro,Claudio Lazarte,Eugen Radu,Armando A. Roque,Nicolas Sanchis-Gual,Etevaldo dos Santos Costa Filho*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-Proca理论在牛顿极限下的静态孤子解，并与自旋-1薛定谔-泊松系统的对应解进行了映射比较，揭示了相对论性与非相对论性描述之间在形态和稳定性上的差异，结论是牛顿极限下存在更丰富的稳定平衡构型谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究爱因斯坦-Proca理论在牛顿极限（ω→μ）下的静态孤子解，并与自旋-1薛定谔-泊松系统的有效描述建立映射关系，澄清相对论性与非相对论性描述之间的差异，特别是由于Proca场的矢量性质和非相对论自旋-1区域的U(3)对称性所带来的复杂特征。

Method: 通过将ℓ=0电Proca星映射为径向极化（刺猬）构型，ℓ=1电Proca星映射为线极化构型，建立两个描述之间的对应关系，并分析基态及相对论性与牛顿解在形态和稳定性上的差异。

Result: 建立了爱因斯坦-Proca理论与自旋-1薛定谔-泊松系统之间的映射关系；澄清了基态方面的一些问题；发现非相对论性理论支持更丰富的稳定平衡构型谱，包括稳定的激发态。

Conclusion: 非相对论性自旋-1系统比相对论性爱因斯坦-Proca理论拥有更丰富的稳定平衡构型谱，包括稳定的激发态。

Abstract: We investigate a representative set of static solitonic solutions of the Einstein-Proca theory in the Newtonian regime, where the field frequency approaches the particle mass, $ω\to μ$, and compare them with the corresponding solutions of the spin-1 Schrödinger-Poisson system, which provides the effective description in this limit. While this correspondence is relatively straightforward in the Einstein-Klein-Gordon case, the vector nature of the Proca field, combined with the enhanced $U(3)$ symmetry of the nonrelativistic spin-1 regime, gives rise to several nontrivial features that require careful analysis. We establish a mapping between the two descriptions by identifying $\ell=0$ electric Proca stars with radially polarized (hedgehog) configurations and $\ell=1$ electric Proca stars with linearly polarized configurations. We further clarify some aspects of the ground state and resolve several apparent discrepancies between relativistic and Newtonian solutions, particularly concerning their morphology and stability properties. An important conclusion of this work is that the nonrelativistic regime supports a richer spectrum of stable equilibrium configurations than the relativistic theory, including stable excited states.

</details>


### [98] [Universality in the Transition from Inspiral to Plunge: High-Accuracy Analytic Solutions and Catastrophe Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13786)
*Ariadna Ribes Metidieri,Béatrice Bonga,Badri Krishnan,José Luis Jaramillo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Kerr时空中极端质量比旋进系统从旋进到坠入的过渡过程，证明该过渡动力学由Painlevé I方程支配，并利用突变理论揭示了其数学结构：赤道轨道对应折叠突变，倾斜轨道对应尖点突变。物理解边界条件唯一选择了Painlevé I方程的三叉戟解，且解析近似相比数值积分具有更优的稳定性和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示极端质量比旋进系统从旋进到坠入过渡动力学普适性背后的数学结构，特别是为何在更复杂的倾斜轨道情形下，过渡动力学仍由与赤道旋进相同的Painlevé I方程支配。

Method: 首先分析Painlevé I方程在缓慢演化准圆形旋进物理解边界条件下的解，证明其唯一选择三叉戟解；然后比较三叉戟解的高精度解析近似与直接数值积分，评估精度和稳定性；最后利用突变理论解释Kerr径向有效势的平衡结构：将赤道轨道和倾斜轨道分别映射到折叠突变和尖点突变的流形结构，证明坠入过渡对应沿突变流形折叠线的缓慢演化。

Result: 1) 物理边界条件唯一选择Painlevé I方程的三叉戟解；2) 解析近似与数值积分精度相当，且在微分和积分下具有更优的稳定性；3) 赤道轨道对应折叠突变，倾斜轨道对应尖点突变，过渡到坠入均对应沿突变流形折叠线的缓慢演化，从而几何化地解释了Painlevé I方程普适出现的根源。

Conclusion: 突变理论为极端质量比旋进系统的坠入过渡动力学提供了统一的几何框架，无论轨道是否倾斜，其普适动力学皆源于突变流形的拓扑结构，这加深了对此类系统非线性动力学本质的理解。

Abstract: We revisit the transition from inspiral to plunge for extreme mass-ratio inspirals on quasi-circular, inclined orbits in Kerr spacetime from the perspective of catastrophe theory. Our goal is to uncover the mathematical structures underlying the universality of the transition dynamics, which remains governed by the same Painlevé I differential equation as for equatorial inspirals despite the additional complexity. We first analyze the solution of the Painlevé I equation selected by the physical boundary conditions of slowly evolving quasi-circular inspiral at early times. We argue that these conditions uniquely select the tritronquée solution of Painlevé I. We then compare existing high-accuracy analytic approximations of the tritronquée solution with direct numerical integrations of the Painlevé I equation, finding comparable accuracy and improved stability under differentiation and integration for the analytic solution. In the second part of this work, we show that the equilibrium structure of the Kerr radial effective potential admits a natural interpretation in terms of catastrophe theory. Equatorial orbits are associated with the fold catastrophe, while inclined orbits are described by the cusp catastrophe. In both cases, the transition to plunge corresponds to slow evolution across fold lines of the catastrophe manifold, providing a geometric explanation for the universal appearance of the Painlevé I equation in the transition dynamics.

</details>


### [99] [Mode decomposition methods for the analysis of black hole ringdown signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13851)
*Andrzej Krolak,Massimo Tinto*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了经验模态分解（EMD）和变分模态分解（VMD）在双黑洞并合后引力波信号环降阶段分析中的适用性，通过蒙特卡洛模拟评估了两种方法分离准正规模（QNM）并估计其频率的能力，结果表明VMD在频率估计精度上显著优于EMD，并提出了改进的瞬时频率估计方法IFAD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从双黑洞并合环降阶段的引力波信号中提取多个准正规模（QNM）对于黑洞光谱学至关重要，但由于信号持续时间短、信噪比（SNR）有限以及模式间干扰，多模式提取仍具挑战性，亟需新的分析方法。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟生成含噪声的环降信号（由成对QNM构成），分别应用EMD和VMD分解信号，通过希尔伯特变换（HT）和提出的新方法IFAD获取瞬时频率，系统评估不同SNR条件下的模式分离与频率估计性能。

Result: EMD和VMD均能分辨多个模式，但VMD在频率估计精度上显著更高；提出的IFAD方法改进了传统希尔伯特变换的频率估计精度；研究量化了双模式可分辨的条件，揭示了频率间隔过近和信号持续时间短带来的局限性。

Conclusion: 该研究对当前及未来地基和空间引力波探测器的高SNR探测具有参考价值，为黑洞光谱学中的多模式提取提供了可行的信号处理方案。

Abstract: The ringdown phase of a binary black hole merger encodes fundamental information about the remnant through its quasinormal mode (QNM) spectrum. Extracting multiple modes from gravitational-wave data is essential for black-hole spectroscopy but remains challenging due to the short duration of the signal, limited signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), and interference between modes. In this work, we investigate the applicability of Empirical Mode Decomposition (EMD) and Variational Mode Decomposition (VMD) to the analysis of post-merger gravitational-wave signals. Using Monte Carlo simulations of noisy ringdown signals composed of pairs of QNMs, we assessed the ability of these methods to separate the two modes and estimate their frequencies. The instantaneous frequency is obtained via the Hilbert transform (HT) and our new proposed method, named instantaneous frequency and amplitude determination (IFAD). We analyze performance over a wide range of SNR values relevant to current and future gravitational-wave detectors. Our results show that both EMD and VMD can resolve multiple modes, and VMD provides significantly more accurate frequency estimates. We also introduce a modified instantaneous frequency estimator that improves accuracy over the Hilbert transform. The study quantifies the conditions under which two-mode resolution is feasible and highlights the limitations imposed by closely spaced mode frequencies and short signal duration. These results are relevant for current observations and for future high-SNR detections expected from space-based and next-generation ground detectors.

</details>


### [100] [Black hole physics within $f(R)$ gravity: Quasi-normal spectra and greybody factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13926)
*Ángel Rincón,Grigoris Panotopoulos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在f(R)引力框架下研究了一个四维黑洞解，通过单一额外参数α刻画与广义相对论的偏离。采用多种互补方法分析了该黑洞的似正规模（标量扰动）和灰体因子，系统研究了参数α、泛音数n和多极数ℓ对似正规频率实部/虚部及灰体因子的影响，并详细讨论了吸收截面、反射系数和透射系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索f(R)引力中修正黑洞解的天体物理相关性质，特别是通过参数α量化与广义相对论的偏差，从而理解修正引力理论对黑洞扰动特征的影响。

Method: 采用三种独立方法研究似正规模：六阶WKB半解析方法、解析表达式以及eikonal极限下的解析表达式。灰体因子计算采用WKB近似方法。系统扫描参数空间（α、n、ℓ）分析各物理量的依赖关系。

Result: 获得了似正规频率随参数α、泛音数n和多极数ℓ的变化规律，以及灰体因子对多极数ℓ和自由参数α的依赖关系，同时给出了吸收截面、反射系数和透射系数的详细分析结果。

Conclusion: f(R)引力中的额外参数α对黑洞的似正规模谱和灰体因子产生显著影响，表明修正引力理论可改变黑洞的扰动特征和辐射性质，这为未来通过引力波观测检验修正引力理论提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate several astrophysical motivated properties of a four-dimensional black hole solution in the framework of $f(R)$ gravity. The model is characterized by a single additional parameter, $α$, which encodes nontrivial deviations from General Relativity. Using this black hole spacetime as the background geometry, we analyze: (i) the quasinormal modes of massless scalar perturbations (employing several complementary methods), and (ii) the greybody factors associated to the propagation of massless test scalar fields. Regarding quasinormal modes, we study the response of black holes to massless scalar perturbations using three independent approaches: the well-established sixth-order WKB semi-analytic method, analytic expressions, and the analytical expression in the eikonal limit. We examine the behavior of both the real and imaginary parts of the quasinormal frequencies as functions of the parameter $α$, the overtone number $n$, and the multipole number $\ell$. In addition to that, we compute the greybody factors within the WKB approximation for various combinations of the relevant parameters. In particular, we focus on the propagation of test massless scalar fields and investigate how the greybody factors depend on the multipole number $\ell$, and the free parameter $α$. The impact of the aforementioned parameters on the absorption cross-section and the reflection and transmission coefficients is studied in detail.

</details>


### [101] [Binary Black Hole Parameter Estimation with Hybrid CNN-Transformer Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13941)
*Panagiotis N. Sakellariou,Spiros V. Georgakopoulos,Sotiris Tasoulis,Vassilis P. Plagianakos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合深度学习策略，结合Transformer编码器和卷积神经网络，对非进动双黑洞系统的内禀和外在参数进行点估计，并在模拟数据和真实引力波事件上展示了良好的预测性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统引力波信号检测依赖模板匹配滤波和符合分析，计算成本高且对模型依赖性强。近年来机器学习/深度学习的进展为信号检测和参数估计提供了新思路，但现有方法仍有改进空间，因此探索混合深度学习架构以提升参数估计性能。

Method: 提出混合深度学习策略，结合Transformer编码器（捕捉长程时序依赖）与卷积神经网络（提取局部特征），对非进动双黑洞系统进行点估计（输出单一最优参数值而非完整后验分布）。

Result: 在含高斯噪声的模拟信号和真实引力波事件上均表现出较强的预测性能和鲁棒性，能够有效估计关键天体物理参数。

Conclusion: 混合Transformer-CNN架构在引力波参数估计任务中具有潜力，点估计方式可作为一种高效的替代方案，为未来深度学习在引力波天文学中的应用提供了新的方向。

Abstract: The detection of gravitational waves has revolutionized our ability to explore fundamental aspects of the Universe. Traditionally, modeled gravitational-wave signals have been identified using template-based matched filtering, followed by coincidence analysis across multiple detectors in the signal-to-noise ratio time series. Recent advances in Machine Learning and Deep Learning have sparked growing interest in their application to both signal detection and parameter estimation. In this study, a hybrid Deep Learning strategy is proposed that leverages the effectiveness of Transformer encoders alongside well-established Convolutional Neural Network architectures in an attempt to estimate the intrinsic and extrinsic parameters of non-precessing binary black hole systems. The primary focus of this work is point estimation, producing single best-fit values for each parameter rather than full posterior distributions. This method is evaluated on both simulated signals embedded in Gaussian noise and real gravitational-wave events, and it demonstrates strong predictive performance and robustness across key astrophysical parameters.

</details>


### [102] [A theory agnostic uniqueness theorem for the Kerr solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14076)
*Joshua Baines*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在不预先假设爱因斯坦方程成立的前提下，利用对称性论证和渐近条件，证明了克尔时空的唯一性，建立了一个与理论无关的唯一性定理，并指出奇点即使在脱离爱因斯坦方程的情况下也可能是不可避免的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望证明克尔解的唯一性不依赖于爱因斯坦方程的具体形式，从而为量子引力或修正引力理论中尝试消除奇异性行为的研究提供理论基础，并与彭罗斯奇点定理形成互补。

Method: 通过构造一组适当的对称性论证和渐近条件，在不假设爱因斯坦方程成立的情况下，推导克尔时空的唯一性。

Result: 建立了一个与理论无关的（theory agnostic）克尔时空唯一性定理。

Conclusion: 即使不预设爱因斯坦方程的有效性，奇点在某些条件下仍然是不可避免的，这补充了彭罗斯奇点定理的结论。

Abstract: In this paper, we show that under a suitable set of symmetry arguments and asymptotic conditions, the uniqueness of the Kerr solution can be proven. None of the conditions considered herein explicitly assume the validity of the Einstein equations. Hence, we are able to construct a theory agnostic uniqueness theorem for the Kerr spacetime. This result has implications for theories of quantum gravity or modified theories of gravity which wish to excise singular behaviour from their corresponding spacetimes. Furthermore, this work is complimentary to Penrose's singularity theorem. While the spacetime considered herein is not as general as that assumed by Penrose, we show that singularities can be unavoidable even when the validity of the Einstein equations is not presupposed.

</details>


### [103] [Refocusing of Wheeler--DeWitt wave functions at inner horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14126)
*Takeshi Chiba,Keiju Murata,Daiki Saito*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 通过求解微超空间近似下的Wheeler-DeWitt方程，研究了双视界双曲黑洞内部的量子引力效应。发现WDW方程存在快子区域（有效势为负），并开发了数值方法在该区域稳定演化波函数。当量子引力参数κ较小时，波包沿经典轨迹传播仅轻微展宽；κ增大时量子效应导致显著展宽。但若初态局域于外视界附近，波包会在内视界附近重新局域化（重聚焦现象）。这表明在外视界处经典化的几何，在内视界处也会重新经典化，内视界的形成在微超空间近似下不受量子引力效应阻碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子引力效应对双视界黑洞（含外视界和内视界）内部区域的影响，特别是内视界的形成是否会被量子效应所阻碍或改变。

Method: 采用微超空间近似（minisuperspace approximation）下的Wheeler-DeWitt方程，针对存在快子区域（有效势为负）的情况开发了数值演化方法，使波函数能稳定穿越该区域并在整个微超空间获得稳定解。

Result: 1) 小κ（弱量子引力）时，波包沿经典轨迹传播，展宽轻微；2) 大κ（强量子引力）时，波包显著展宽；3) 发现"重聚焦"现象：初态局域于外视界的波包，在内视界附近重新恢复局域化。

Conclusion: 在微超空间近似下，双曲黑洞的内视界形成不受量子引力效应阻碍。若外视界处几何是经典的，则内视界处几何也会重新变为经典，量子效应不会破坏内视界的形成。

Abstract: We study quantum gravitational effects inside hyperbolic black holes with both outer and inner horizons by solving the Wheeler--DeWitt (WDW) equation in the minisuperspace approximation. The WDW equation contains a tachyonic region where the effective potential becomes negative. We develop a numerical method that consistently evolves the wave function across this region and obtain stable solutions throughout the entire minisuperspace. For small values of the parameter $κ$, which controls the strength of quantum gravitational effects, the wave packet propagates along the classical trajectory with only mild quantum spreading. As $κ$ increases, enhanced quantum effects lead to significant spreading of the wave packet during its propagation. Nevertheless, when the initial state is localized near the outer horizon, the wave packet becomes localized again in the vicinity of the inner horizon. We refer to this recovery of localization as a refocusing phenomenon. This result suggests that, if the geometry is classical near the outer horizon, it becomes classical again near the inner horizon. Within the minisuperspace approximation, inner-horizon formation is not obstructed by quantum gravitational effects.

</details>


### [104] [Note on the local calculation of decoherence of quantum superposition in the static black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14178)
*Ran Li,Zhong-Xiao Man,Jin Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schwarzschild和Reissner-Nordström黑洞中静态粒子的量子空间叠加态的退相干。通过将粒子视为与量子标量场耦合的局域经典源，利用相干态生成重述了DSW思想实验，并导出了用Wightman函数表示的退相干泛函的局域表达式。在长时间极限下，退相干率由Wightman函数的低频行为刻画。采用渐近匹配方法计算了Boulware、Unruh和Hartle-Hawking真空中的Wightman函数解析表达式。结果表明退相干行为依赖于环境场的量子态：Boulware真空下静态叠加的退相干为零，而Unruh和Hartle-Hawking真空中与霍金辐射相关的热效应可诱导非零退相干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究黑洞背景下量子空间叠加态的退相干机制，特别是量子场真空态（Boulware、Unruh、Hartle-Hawking）对退相干行为的影响，以及霍金辐射的热效应在退相干过程中的作用。

Method: 将粒子视为局域经典源，通过相干态生成重述DSW退相干思想实验；导出退相干泛函的Wightman函数局域表达式；利用渐近匹配方法计算三种真空态下的Wightman函数解析形式。

Result: 退相干率在长时间极限下由Wightman函数的低频行为决定；Boulware真空下静态叠加退相干为零；Unruh和Hartle-Hawking真空中霍金辐射热效应可诱导非零退相干。

Conclusion: 黑洞环境中量子空间叠加的退相干行为依赖于环境量子场的真空态选择，热效应（如霍金辐射）是关键因素，不同真空态导致截然不同的退相干特性。

Abstract: We investigate the decoherence of a quantum spatial superposition of a static particle in Schwarzschild and Reissner-Nordström black holes. By treating the particle as a localized classical source coupled to a quantum scalar field, we reformulate the decoherence process in the Danielson-Satishchandran-Wald (DSW) gedankenexperiment through coherent state generation and derive the local expression for the decoherence functional in terms of the Wightman function. In the long-time limit, the decoherence rate is shown to be characterized by the low-frequency behavior of the Wightman function. We then employ the asymptotic matching method to calculate the analytical expressions of the Wightman functions in the Boulware, Unruh, and Hartle-Hawking vacua. We show that the decoherence behavior depends on the quantum state of the environmental field. While the Boulware vacuum gives vanishing decoherence for a static superposition, the thermal effects associated with Hawking radiation in the Unruh and Hartle-Hawking vacua can induce nonvanishing decoherence.

</details>


### [105] [Majorana Neutrinos in Sandwich Wave Spacetimes: Flavor Modulation and Helicity Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14180)
*Yorgo Senikoglu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了巨大马约拉纳中微子在精确引力沙漏波背景下的传播，发现引力波脉冲会破坏质量本征态的运动学对齐，产生永久残余相位位移，并导致中微子从左手态向右手态转变，从而在风味和螺旋性上留下引力记忆效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究引力波对中微子传播的影响，特别是引力记忆效应在风味和螺旋性上的可观测信号。

Method: 采用Takagi分解来解耦矩阵场方程，分析巨大马约拉纳中微子在精确引力沙漏波背景中的传播行为。

Result: 局域脉冲破坏了质量本征态的运动学对齐，在波前横向轮廓上留下永久残余相位位移；波的不对称性直接耦合到马约拉纳自旋张量，诱导左手态到右手态的跃迁；残余漂移参数导致手征性极化，在横向动量平面上引入强各向异性。

Conclusion: 经过的引力波会在中微子束的风味和螺旋性上留下修饰，作为引力记忆效应的标志。

Abstract: We present the propagation of massive Majorana neutrinos crossing an exact gravitational sandwich wave background. By employing a Takagi factorization to decouple the matrix field equations, We show that the localized pulse breaks the kinematic alignment of the mass eigenstates as they propagate through the wave zone, leaving a permanent residual phase shift across the transverse profile of the wavefront. The wave asymmetry couples directly to the Majorana spin tensor, forcing a transition from left-handed to right-handed configurations. Behind the wave tail, the residual drift parameters act to polarize the state's chirality, introducing a strong anisotropy across the transverse momentum plane. These results show that a passing gravitational wave leaves a modification on both the flavor and helicity of a neutrino beam, as a sign of the gravitational memory effect.

</details>


### [106] [Sachs-Wolfe effect as a smoking gun for cosmological gravitational wave backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14379)
*Giorgio Mentasti,Leon Vidal,Quentin Baghi,Carlo Contaldi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了宇宙大尺度结构导致的Sachs-Wolfe效应对引力波背景的影响，发现当引力波能量密度参数Ω_GW≳10^{-10}时，该效应会在引力波背景中留下可探测的各向异性和谱畸变信号，且这些信号与CMB中的SW信号存在关联，可作为检验引力波背景原初起源的"确凿证据"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Sachs-Wolfe效应对宇宙学引力波背景的影响，评估其可探测性以及对引力波背景理论模型参数估计可能造成的偏差。

Method: 通过理论分析计算SW效应对原初引力波背景的修正，包括各向异性和谱畸变，并评估LISA+Taiji等空间干涉仪网络的探测能力，以及分析GW各向异性与CMB的交叉关联。

Result: 当Ω_GW≳10^{-10}时，SW效应导致的各向异性和谱畸变可被空间干涉仪网络探测到；该效应在频谱高频端尤为增强；原初引力波背景中的SW信号与CMB中的SW信号存在可交叉关联的特征。

Conclusion: SW效应在强引力波背景下不可忽略，忽视该效应会偏置理论模型估计；GW与CMB的SW信号交叉关联是确认引力波背景原初起源的强有力手段。

Abstract: The Sachs-Wolfe (SW) effect, arising from large-scale structures in the universe, modifies the frequencies of gravitational waves (GWs) sourced by a cosmological background. We show that for backgrounds with $Ω_{\rm GW}\gtrsim 10^{-10}$, this effect imprints anisotropies and spectral distortions that can be detectable with a network of space-based interferometers (such as LISA + Taiji) and, if not taken into account, may bias the estimate of the theoretical model of the GW background. The effect is particularly enhanced in the high-frequency end of the spectrum. The SW-induced anisotropies and spectral distortions present in a GW background sourced at primordial times will correlate with the SW signature present in the CMB. Any detection of a cross-correlation between the GW anisotropies and the CMB at large scales is therefore a smoking gun for confirming the primordial nature of the background.

</details>


### [107] [Dynamical tidal response of neutron stars via scattering amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14405)
*M. V. S. Saketh,Suprovo Ghosh,Nils Andersson*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在场论有效理论框架下系统定义了中子星的动力学潮汐响应，通过将散射振幅从有效理论与紫外理论（恒星微扰理论）匹配，得到了与静态极限、共振模式及引力波耗散诱导的阻尼振荡行为一致的动力学潮汐响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波物理中的一个关键挑战是区分双致密星并合中致密天体的性质（特别是黑洞与中子星）。中子星比黑洞具有更强的潮汐响应，其静态和动力学方面都与内部物理直接相关。然而，在广义相对论中定义中子星的潮汐响应存在坐标歧义性以及将恒星响应与双星动力学和引力波波形相关联的复杂性。

Method: 在世界线有效场论框架内系统定义中子星的动力学潮汐响应，将问题归结为孤立中子星对引力波的散射。散射振幅分别在有效理论（使用标准量子场论技术）和恒星微扰理论（紫外理论，求解广义相对论框架下恒星内部的耦合度规和物质微扰方程，并与外部解析的MST解匹配）中计算，通过匹配两者得到动力学潮汐响应。

Result: 匹配得到的动力学潮汐响应与已知预期一致：包括静态极限、中子星共振模式附近的行为，以及正确恢复了由引力波耗散诱导的主振荡模式的虚部（阻尼部分）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法为系统定义中子星动力学潮汐响应提供了理论框架，并讨论了有效场论和微扰理论两方面未来可能的改进方向。

Abstract: A key challenge of gravitational-wave physics is distinguishing the nature of compact objects involved in binary coalescences, particularly whether they are black holes or neutron stars. Neutron stars are distinguished from black holes by a stronger tidal response, with both static and dynamical aspects directly linked to their rich internal physics. Measurements of the tidal response through gravitational observations constrains the neutron-star equation of state and provides insight into the physics of high-density matter. However, defining the tidal response of neutron stars in general relativity is challenging due to coordinate ambiguities and the complexity of connecting the star's response to binary dynamics and the associated gravitational waveforms. In this paper, we show how the dynamical tidal response of a neutron star can be systematically defined within the worldline effective field theory (EFT) framework, connecting the problem to gravitational-wave scattering off an isolated neutron star. These scattering amplitudes are computed both within the EFT, using standard quantum field-theory techniques, and within stellar perturbation theory (the corresponding ultraviolet theory), where the coupled metric and matter perturbation equations are solved in the stellar interior within general relativity and matched to the analytical Mano-Suzuki-Takasugi (MST) solutions in the exterior. We match the scattering amplitude between effective theory and the ultraviolet theory to obtain the dynamical tidal response. We show the result to be consistent with known expectations, such as the static limit and the behaviour near the neutron star's resonant modes, while also recovering the imaginary part of the dominant oscillation mode induced by gravitational-wave dissipation. We conclude with a discussion of potential future improvements within both the EFT and the perturbation theory.

</details>


### [108] [Spinning test particles in the spacetime of a global monopole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14509)
*Morteza Mohseni,Saurya Das*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon框架下，研究了全局单极子时空中自旋测试粒子的运动，获得了运动方程的精确通解，证明了系统的完全可积性，并分析了径向和平面运动等特殊情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究全局单极子背景引力场中自旋粒子的动力学行为，理解自旋-曲率耦合对粒子运动轨迹的影响。

Method: 基于Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon方程，利用时空对称性（Killing矢量）导出守恒量，将运动方程解耦为极角与方位角的函数表达，并通过可积性分析得到精确解析解。

Result: 获得了自旋粒子运动方程的精确通解；粒子轨迹、动量和自旋均可由三个关于极角和方位角的特定函数表示；系统完全可积；得到了非测地线轨迹，并与无自旋粒子的测地线运动进行了对比。

Conclusion: 自旋粒子在全局单极子时空中沿非测地线运动，其轨道偏离由自旋-曲率耦合决定，且该系统在数学上具有完全可积性。

Abstract: We investigate the motion of spinning test particles in the spacetime of a global monopole in the framework of the Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon equations. By making use of the symmetries of the spacetime, we obtain a general exact solution to the equations of motion. We show that the particle's trajectories, momenta, and spin can be expressed in terms of three specific functions of the polar and azimuthal angles. We also show that the system is completely integrable. We obtain the general non-geodesic trajectory of the particle, and also examine the particular cases of radial and planar motion. We compare the non-geodesic trajectories of a spinning particle with a non-spinning particle.

</details>


### [109] [Modeling dark matter as self-bound quantum liquid droplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14537)
*Abdelaali Boudjemaa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了暗物质以超稀释量子玻色混合物在Lee-Huang-Yang修正下形成的自束缚量子液滴存在的可能性，通过非相对论自洽Hartree-Fock-Bogoliubov理论和流体力学方法推导了扩展状态方程，并发现暗物质晕的关键参数对种间相互作用和量子涨落强度敏感，最后与银河系旋转曲线的观测数据进行了比较验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索暗物质能否以超稀释量子玻色混合物在Lee-Huang-Yang修正下形成的自束缚量子液滴形式存在，为玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚暗物质模型提供新的理论依据。

Method: 采用非相对论自洽Hartree-Fock-Bogoliubov理论和流体力学方法，推导了包含Lee-Huang-Yang修正的扩展状态方程；通过分析量子流体动力学方程的小扰动，研究液滴状银河系晕的稳定性和动力学演化；与银河系旋转曲线的观测数据进行比较。

Result: 暗物质晕的关键参数（密度、质量和半径）对种间相互作用强度和量子涨落强度高度敏感；所得理论预测与银河系旋转曲线的观测数据具有可比性。

Conclusion: 超稀释量子玻色混合物形成的自束缚量子液滴可以作为暗物质的一种可行候选形态，该模型能够较好地解释银河系旋转曲线等观测特征。

Abstract: The Bose-Einstein condensate dark matter model, where dark matter can be thought of as a non-relativistic, Newtonian gravitational condensate, has recently attracted a great deal of interest. In the present study, we explore the possibility that the dark matter could exist in the form of a self-bound quantum droplet formed by ultradilute quantum Bose mixtures under the action of Lee-Huang-Yang corrections at zero temperature. To this end, we derive an extended equation of state by using the nonrelativistic self-consistent Hartree-Fock-Bogoliubov theory and the hydrodynamic approach. The solutions of the obtained equations of state show that the key parameters of the dark matter halos such as the density, mass, and radius are sensitive to the interspecies interaction and to the quantum fluctuation strength. The stability and the dynamical evolution of the droplet Galactic halos are analyzed by considering small perturbations of the quantum hydrodynamical equations. In order to increase the reliability of our predictions we compare them with some observed data for the Galactic rotation curves.

</details>


### [110] [Searching for Extra Dimensions with Gravitational Waves: Dark-Siren Constraints from GWTC-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14549)
*Anson Chen,Jun Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用GWTC-4中的141个致密双星并合事件，结合GLADE+星系目录，通过暗汽笛方法对高维引力波传播进行层级贝叶斯分析。在给定哈勃常数和交叉尺度先验下，得到时空维度D=4.38^{+1.91}_{-1.01}（68%置信区间），交叉尺度R_c约束较差且倾向于先验上界。结果表明当前观测与四维广义相对论一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维引力理论预言引力波可传播到额外空间维度，导致振幅衰减偏离广义相对论预言。通过测量引力波光度距离可探测时空维度，因此有必要利用现有引力波数据对高维引力波传播进行观测约束。

Method: 采用膜世界情景启发的唯象参数化方案，用时空维度数D和交叉尺度R_c表征高维修正。对GWTC-4中141个致密双星并合事件，结合GLADE+星系目录的视线方向星系信息，进行层级贝叶斯分析（暗汽笛方法）。

Result: 在先验H0∈[65,77] km/s/Mpc和log(R_c/Mpc)∈[2.7,4.0]下，得到D=4.38^{+1.91}_{-1.01}（68%置信区间）。R_c的后验分布积累在先验上界附近，约束较差。D的约束对R_c的先验范围敏感。

Conclusion: 这是首个利用GWTC-4暗汽笛方法对高维引力波传播的约束结果，表明当前观测仍与四维广义相对论一致，高维修正尚未被探测到。

Abstract: Higher-dimensional theories of gravity predict that gravitational waves (GWs) can propagate into extra spatial dimensions, leading to modified amplitude damping over cosmological distances. Measurements of GW luminosity distances therefore provide a unique probe of the dimensionality of spacetime. In this work, we constrain higher-dimensional GW propagation using the dark-siren method with the Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalog 4.0 (GWTC-4). We adopt a phenomenological parameterization motivated by braneworld scenarios, in which deviations from General Relativity are characterized by the spacetime dimension number $D$ and a crossover scale $R_c$ governing the transition between four- and higher-dimensional gravity. We perform a hierarchical Bayesian analysis combining 141 compact binary coalescences from GWTC-4 with line-of-sight galaxy information from the GLADE+ catalog. For a prior $H_0 \in [65,77]\ {\rm km~s^{-1}Mpc^{-1}}$ and $\log(R_c/{\rm Mpc}) \in [2.7,4.0]$, we obtain $D = 4.38^{+1.91}_{-1.01}$ (68\% credible interval). We also find that the inferred posterior distribution of $R_c$ accumulates near the upper prior boundary, indicating that the crossover scale remains poorly constrained by current observations. We further show that the inferred constraint on $D$ depends sensitively on the assumed prior range of $R_c$, which determines the characteristic distance scale at which deviations from General Relativity become significant. Our results provide the first GWTC-4 dark-siren constraints on higher-dimensional GW propagation and demonstrate that current observations remain consistent with four-dimensional General Relativity.

</details>
