### [111] [Tantalum as a base material for superconducting integrated circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13750)
*Evgeniy V. Zikiy,Nikita S. Smirnov,Stanislav A. Kotenkov,Ilya A. Stepanov,Julia A. Agafonova,Daria A. Moskaleva,Aleksei R. Matanin,Anton I. Ivanov,Dmitry O. Moskalev,Julia A. Kurochkina,Aleksander V. Andriyash,Ilya A. Rodionov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了钽薄膜在超导量子电路中的应用进展，涵盖薄膜生长、相选择机制、电路制造工艺及低温异常行为等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钽作为新一代量子电路材料在超导谐振器和量子比特中展现出突破性性能，但其薄膜多态性、晶体结构差异及低温异常行为尚未被系统梳理，亟需一篇综述来整合现有研究成果并指出开放问题。

Method: 系统综述了钽薄膜在不同衬底上的生长与相选择机制、超导电路的制造工艺与性能，并结合第一性原理理论计算进行分析。

Result: 总结了钽薄膜的多晶型结构（如α相和β相）对超导性能的影响，以及钽基电路在低温实验中表现出的区别于铝基和铌基电路的独特特征。

Conclusion: 指出了当前仍待解决的开放问题，包括β相杂质的作用、氢氟酸溶液对链特性的影响，以及α-钽链在低温下的异常行为。

Abstract: The performance of superconducting integrated circuits for quantum applications is fundamentally limited by material-related losses. Tantalum, as an emerging material for next-generation quantum circuits, has attracted considerable attention in recent years after demonstrating breakthrough performance in both superconducting microwave resonators and qubits. Concurrently, a growing body of work is devoted to the operation of tantalum-based circuits and related fabrication techniques. This interest is further stimulated by tantalum thin films polymorphism resulting in a variety of its crystalline structure, superconducting properties, coherence, etc. Furthermore, tantalum circuits exhibit distinctive features in cryogenic experiments, which have not been observed in aluminum- or niobium-based ones. In this review, we summarize the recent research of tantalum thin films growth and phase selection mechanisms on various substrates, key aspects of fabrication and performance of superconducting circuit, including a material first-principles theoretical study. In conclusion, we address a number of open issues, including the role of \b{eta}-phase impurities, the effect of hydrofluoric acid solutions on chain characteristics, and the anomalous behavior of α-tantalum chains at cryogenic temperatures.

</details>


### [112] [Computational regimes in matrix-product-state-based quantum trajectory simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13779)
*Aaron Sander,Simon Cichy,Martin Eigel,Jens Eisert,Maximilian Fröhlich,Tom Peham,Robert Wille*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于矩阵乘积态（MPS）的量子轨迹模拟成本分解框架，将总成本分解为单轨迹内存、单轨迹运行时间和采样开销三个维度。通过引入键维膨胀因子α和采样膨胀因子κ两个无量纲量，量化了不同随机解纠缠方式在轨迹复杂度和统计收敛之间的权衡，并提供了从小型预模拟中提取(α,κ)的实用协议。结果表明，最优解纠缠方式依赖于噪声强度、时间步长分辨率、系统规模和并行度等硬件相关约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于轨迹的张量网络方法中，总成本不仅取决于单轨迹的纠缠增长或键维，还取决于统计采样开销。这两个因素之间的相互作用尚不明确，导致无法有效选择最优的随机解纠缠方式以降低总计算成本。

Method: 提出了一个成本分解框架，将总成本分解为三个维度；引入键维膨胀因子α和采样膨胀因子κ来量化不同解纠缠方式下的成本权衡；设计了从小型预模拟中提取(α,κ)的实用协议；在多种噪声通道上进行基准测试。

Result: 物理等价的随机解纠缠方式并不必然降低总成本，而是在轨迹复杂度和统计收敛之间重新分配成本；最优解纠缠方式随噪声强度、时间步长分辨率、系统规模和可用并行度而变化；提供了决策图来指导不同硬件条件下的解纠缠选择。

Conclusion: 解纠缠方式的选择应被视为一个硬件感知的模拟设计问题，而非单纯优化轨迹纠缠的固有属性。

Abstract: Efficient simulation of open quantum systems is central to modeling noisy quantum hardware and many-body dynamics. In trajectory-based tensor network methods, cost is often associated with trajectory-level quantities such as entanglement growth or bond dimension. However, the total cost of a fixed-accuracy simulation also depends on statistical sampling, and the interplay between per-trajectory complexity and sampling effort remains poorly understood. Here we introduce a cost-resolved framework for matrix product state (MPS)-based quantum trajectory simulations that decomposes total cost into memory per trajectory, runtime per trajectory, and sampling effort. We show that physically equivalent stochastic unravelings of the same Lindblad dynamics do not necessarily reduce total cost, but instead redistribute cost between trajectory complexity and statistical convergence. This trade-off is quantified by two dimensionless inflation factors: a bond dimension inflation $α$ and a sampling inflation $κ$, which together determine the preferred unraveling under hardware-dependent memory and parallelism constraints. We provide a practical protocol for extracting $(α,κ)$ from modest pilot simulations and demonstrate it using benchmarks across multiple noise channels. The resulting decision maps show that the computationally favorable unraveling can change with noise strength, time-step resolution, system size, and available parallelism. These results establish unraveling choice as a hardware-aware simulation design problem rather than an intrinsic optimization of trajectory entanglement alone.

</details>


### [113] [Universal Crossovers of Stabilizer Entropy Beyond Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13810)
*Reyhaneh Khasseh,E. A. Ramirez Trino,M. A. Rajabpour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过精确求解有限范围自旋链在Rényi指数为1/2时的稳定子熵（SRE），揭示了从临界到非临界行为的普适交叉标度律，包括周期链的体积律与有限尺寸交叉、子系统熵的边界贡献，以及XX点处独特的标度行为，为稳定子熵的量子场论描述提供了精确晶格基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子熵作为量子多体系统中非稳定化性质（nonstabilizerness）的探针，其临界点之外的标度结构相比纠缠熵尚不清晰。最近的场论方法表明稳定子熵包含普适临界数据和边界敏感项，但这些结构如何扩展到有质量和交叉区域仍是开放问题。

Method: 针对Rényi指数为1/2的一大类有限范围自旋链，推导出全周期链和无限链有限区间的精确有限尺寸公式，使从临界到非临界行为的普适交叉在解析上变得可处理。通过精确的稳定子熵对应关系，将标度理论扩展到内部XY约化、有限范围自旋链和Cluster-Ising代表系统。

Result: 周期几何中，熵在远离临界点时服从体积律，并展现出由系统尺寸和关联长度竞争控制的普适有限尺寸交叉。大尺度SRE密度在场调临界线上形成尖点，XX端点由与饱和点相关的不同标度区支配。子系统几何中，区间熵将体临界行为与有限区域切割无限链产生的边界贡献分离开来，从临界到有质量行为的交叉编码在边界常数和由关联长度控制的普适函数中。

Conclusion: 该工作为稳定子熵在孤立共形点之外的量子场论描述提供了精确晶格基准，建立了稳定子熵普适标度理论的基础框架。

Abstract: Stabilizer Rényi entropy has emerged as a probe of nonstabilizerness in quantum many-body systems, but its scaling structure beyond critical points remains poorly understood compared with entanglement entropy. Recent field-theory approaches indicate that stabilizer entropy contains universal critical data and boundary-sensitive terms, raising the question of how these structures extend into massive and crossover regimes. We address this problem for a broad class of finite-range spin chains at Rényi index one-half. We derive exact finite-size formulas for both full periodic chains and finite intervals of the infinite chain, making the universal crossover from critical to noncritical behavior analytically accessible. In periodic geometry, the entropy obeys a volume law away from criticality and exhibits a universal finite-size crossover controlled by the competition between system size and correlation length. We also show that the large-scale SRE density develops a cusp across the field-tuned critical line, while the XX endpoint is governed by a distinct scaling regime associated with the saturation point. In the subsystem geometry, the interval entropy separates bulk critical behavior from boundary contributions generated by the way the finite region cuts the infinite chain. The crossover from critical to massive behavior is then encoded in boundary constants and universal functions controlled by the correlation length. Through exact stabilizer-entropy correspondences, the scaling theory extends to internal XY reductions, Finite-range spin chains, and Cluster--Ising representatives. Our results provide an exact lattice benchmark for the emerging QFT description of stabilizer entropy beyond isolated conformal points.

</details>


### [114] [Aligning Quantum Operators with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13811)
*Rogerio Feris,Yunchao Liu,Pengyuan Li,Hang Hua,David Kremer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将量子酉算子映射到LLM潜在空间的方法，实现量子和语言输入的统一建模，在Clifford+T电路综合任务上取得与最先进方法竞争的结果，并支持语言条件综合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在数学和符号推理方面表现出色，但它们本质上无法理解和推理量子表示（如酉矩阵），需要弥合这一差距。

Method: 将酉算子映射到LLM的潜在空间中，实现量子和语言输入的统一建模，在Pauli旋转门集上的Clifford+T电路综合任务中实现。

Result: 与最先进方法竞争的结果，随训练数据量一致扩展且无饱和迹象，支持语言条件综合（训练中未见过的门约束可通过自然语言指定）。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子感知基础模型铺平了道路，对量子编译和算法发现具有潜在影响。

Abstract: Can Large Language Models (LLMs) understand and reason about quantum operators? Despite their remarkable capabilities in mathematics and symbolic reasoning, LLMs remain inherently blind to quantum representations such as unitary matrices. In this work, we take a step toward bridging this gap by introducing an approach that maps unitary operators into the latent space of an LLM, enabling unified modeling over quantum and linguistic inputs. We instantiate this idea on Clifford+T circuit synthesis over a Pauli rotation gate set, where our model achieves results competitive with state-of-the-art methods and scales consistently with training data, with no signs of saturation. Our approach further enables language-conditioned synthesis, allowing gate constraints unseen during training to be specified directly in natural language. This work suggests a path toward quantum--aware foundation models that can natively interpret and reason about quantum operations, which could have broader implications reaching across quantum compilation and algorithm discovery.

</details>


### [115] [Calibrated Helstrom geometry on the Bloch ball via Connes spectral distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13824)
*Kaushlendra Kumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在有限标量-量子比特-标量模型中，Connes谱距离能够恢复量子比特态的等先验Helstrom迹距离几何。两个标量参考扇区通过恒等狄拉克链各向同性地耦合到量子比特块，使得完整的布洛赫球（包括混合态）从有限谱度量继承其标准弦迹距离几何。标量扇区距离起校准作用：确定单个链长，满足勾股一致性关系，并重建中间扇区尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Connes谱距离与非交换几何中量子态几何（特别是等先验Helstrom迹距离）之间的具体联系，为量子信息理论提供非交换几何的几何解释。

Method: 构建一个有限标量-量子比特-标量三扇区模型，其中两个标量参考扇区通过恒等狄拉克链耦合到中间的量子比特块，分析该模型下的Connes谱距离结构。

Result: 完整的布洛赫球（包括混合态）从有限谱度量继承了标准弦迹距离几何；标量扇区距离可以确定狄拉克链长、满足勾股关系并重建中间扇区尺度。

Conclusion: Connes谱距离在特定模型下能够恢复量子比特态的Helstrom迹距离几何，标量参考扇区在几何校准中起关键作用。

Abstract: We show that the equal-prior Helstrom trace-distance geometry of qubit states is recovered from Connes spectral distance in a finite scalar-qubit-scalar model. The two scalar reference sectors couple isotropically to the qubit block through identity Dirac links, so that the full Bloch ball, including mixed states, inherits its standard chordal trace-distance geometry from the finite spectral metric. The scalar-sector distances serve a distinct calibration role: they determine the individual link lengths, satisfy a Pythagorean consistency relation, and reconstruct the middle-sector scale.

</details>


### [116] [Local correlations in long-range dual-unitary kicked Hamiltonian chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13857)
*Vladimir Al. Osipov,Marc Cedric Spyra,Jana Carolina Schumann,Thomas Guhr,Boris Gutkin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于Hadamard矩阵对，构建了具有长程相互作用的双幺正 kicked 自旋链家族，发现局域两点关联沿修正光锥边缘传播，并可用解析方法推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双幺正模型仅允许最近邻相互作用，限制了对长程相互作用系统中双幺正动力学的理解。作者希望突破这一限制，构造具有长程相互作用的双幺正 kicked 自旋链。

Method: 基于一对Hadamard矩阵构造了具有长程相互作用的双幺正 kicked 自旋链家族，以具有次近邻相互作用的kicked Ising链为例进行说明。

Result: 局域两点关联沿修正后的光锥边缘|n| = r|t|传播（r为相互作用范围），且对于局域支撑的算符可解析推导。

Conclusion: 成功将双幺正动力学推广到长程相互作用系统，为研究非平衡量子多体动力学提供了新的可解析求解模型。

Abstract: Many-body Floquet models with exact space--time symmetry, such as the kicked Ising spin chain (KIC), provide natural examples of systems with dual-unitary dynamics. The requirement of exact space--time symmetry is, however, highly restrictive, as it permits only nearest-neighbor interactions. Based on a pair of Hadamard matrices, we construct a wide family of dual-unitary kicked spin chains with long-range interactions. We show that local two-point correlations in such models propagate along the light-cone edges \( |n| = r|t| \), where \(r\) is the interaction range, and can be derived analytically for operators with local support. This approach is illustrated using the example of a kicked Ising spin chain with next-to-next-neighbor interactions.

</details>


### [117] [Compact graphs and quantum automorphisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13928)
*Pedro Baptista,Gabriel Coutinho,Chris Godsil,Simon Schmidt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子紧图的概念，通过评估量子自同构群在状态上的基本魔法酉矩阵，生成了一个位于经典自同构多面体和完全分数自同构多面体之间的双随机矩阵闭凸集。主要结果是量子紧图本质上等同于经典紧图，即量子紧图是经典的。同时建立了与量子轨道代数的联系，获得了经典与量子紧性的层次结构，并通过交换子恢复了紧性的经典结论，提出了广义传递性和距离传递性的量子类似物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧图是指分数自同构多面体没有真正分数顶点的图。本文旨在建立这一概念的量子类比，探索量子对称性如何细化经典的紧性理论。

Method: 通过评估量子自同构群的基本魔法酉矩阵在状态上的作用，生成双随机矩阵的闭凸集；将该集合与量子轨道代数相联系；通过交换子理论恢复经典紧性的结论。

Result: 量子紧图本质上等价于经典紧图，即量子紧图必然是经典紧图。建立了经典与量子紧性的层次结构，并提出了广义传递性和距离传递性的量子类似物。

Conclusion: 量子紧图的概念在本质上是经典的，但该框架为刻画量子对称性如何不增强经典紧性提供了新视角，同时为量子版本的传递性概念奠定了基础。

Abstract: Compact graphs are graphs for which the fractional automorphism polytope has no genuinely fractional vertices. This paper proposes a quantum analogue of this idea by evaluating the fundamental magic unitary of the quantum automorphism group on states, which we show to produce a closed convex set of doubly stochastic matrices sitting between the classical automorphism polytope and the full fractional automorphism polytope. Our main result is that the natural quantum analogue of compactness is classical, that is, a quantum compact graph is classically compact.
  We also relate this set to the quantum orbital algebra and obtain a hierarchy of classical and quantum compactness pseudo notions. The framework recovers familiar consequences of compactness through commutants and suggests quantum analogues of generous transitivity and distance-transitivity. We also isolate examples and open problems indicating where quantum symmetries may strictly refine the classical compactness theory.

</details>


### [118] [Quantum Simulation of Spin-Dependent Electron Transfer in a Synthetic Chiral Lattice with a Trapped Ion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13930)
*Yi Li,Chuyuan Chen,Xingyu Zhao,Zihan Xie,Min Jiang,Xinhua Peng,Han Pu,Lyuzhou Ye,Yao Wang,Guozhen Zhang,Yiheng Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用囚禁离子实现了供体-手性桥-受体模型中自旋相关电子转移的可编程量子模拟，实验验证了手性诱导自旋选择性效应的微观起源——手性结构内的路径干涉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性诱导自旋选择性效应的微观起源（路径干涉机制）尚未明确，且缺乏精确可调参数的实验验证手段。

Method: 将桥编码于囚禁离子内态中，实现可调最近邻和次近邻耦合；供体与受体态通过旁观玻色子运动模式耦合，进行量子模拟。

Result: 观察到桥内自旋相关干涉，以及供体到受体转移动力学的自旋依赖性，该依赖性受耦合参数的振幅和相位控制。

Conclusion: 自旋相关路径间的干涉是自旋相关转移的微观起源，并为多能级和玻色子自由度的复杂手性晶格量子模拟开辟了道路。

Abstract: Electron transfer through chiral structures can exhibit spin asymmetry, known as the chiral-induced spin selectivity effect, whose microscopic origin remains an open question. While path-interference within the chiral moiety has been proposed as a key mechanism, its experimental validation requires precise and versatile tunability of system parameters. Here we implement a programmable quantum simulation of spin-dependent electron transfer in a donor--chiral-bridge--acceptor model using a trapped ion. The bridge is encoded in internal states of the ion with tunable nearest- and next-nearest-neighbor couplings, while donor and acceptor states are coupled via a spectator bosonic motional mode. We observe spin-dependent interference within the bridge, and further reveal spin-dependence in donor-to-acceptor transfer dynamics, controlled by amplitude and phase of the coupling parameter. Our results identify interference among spin-dependent pathways as a microscopic origin of spin-dependent transfer, and open a route toward quantum simulations of complex chiral lattices with multi-level and bosonic degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [119] [An integrated ultrahigh vacuum cluster tool for diamond surface science and single nitrogen-vacancy center measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13961)
*Zhiyang Yuan,Sorawis Sangtawesin,Lila V. H. Rodgers,Kalliope Zervas,James J. Allred,Jared Rovny,Patryk Gumann,Nathalie P. de Leon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一套定制设计的超高真空（UHV）集群工具，用于研究浅层氮-空位（NV）中心，该工具将金刚石表面制备、表征和单NV中心动力学测量集成在同一平台内，实现了表面化学与NV自旋/电荷特性的直接关联研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅层NV中心对表面环境高度敏感，而现有研究缺乏将表面制备、表征与量子测量集成在同一受控环境中的平台，导致难以建立表面化学与NV自旋/电荷特性之间的直接因果关系。

Method: 设计并搭建了一套UHV集群工具，包含表面科学腔室（用于可控表面改性与分析）和低温共聚焦显微镜腔室（用于NV自旋与光学测量），两腔室在超高真空下互联。

Result: 实现了在同一集成平台上对金刚石表面进行原位制备、表征以及单NV中心自旋和光学性质的测量，能够直接关联表面化学与NV量子特性。

Conclusion: 该平台为系统研究表面诱导的NV中心退相干机制和电荷动力学提供了通用工具，并为面向量子传感应用的可重复表面工程建立了可行路径。

Abstract: We present a custom-designed ultrahigh vacuum (UHV) cluster tool developed for studying shallow nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond, enabling in situ diamond surface preparation, characterization, and single NV center dynamics measurements within a single connected platform. The system combines a surface science chamber for controlled surface modification and analysis with a cryogenic confocal microscope chamber dedicated to NV spin and optical measurements. This integrated approach enables a direct correlation between diamond surface chemistry and the resulting NV spin and charge properties. The instrument provides a versatile platform for systematic studies of surface-induced decoherence mechanisms and charge dynamics for shallow NV centers, and establishes a pathway toward reproducible surface engineering for quantum sensing applications.

</details>


### [120] [Implementation of two-qubit Rydberg operations on neutral Rb-87 atoms in systems with different intermediate states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13975)
*I. V. Iukhnovets,O. V. Bychkova,I. B. Bobrov,A. P. Gordeev,M. Y. Goloshchapov,G. I. Struchalin,S. S. Straupe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验实现了中性原子（⁸⁷Rb）的两种双量子比特门方案：一种基于5P₁/₂中间能级并局部施加第二束Rydberg激光，另一种基于6P₃/₂能级并将原子移入单独区域施加双束Rydberg激光。通过Julia数值模拟发现空间构型对保真度的影响大于中间能级选择，最终实验采用6P₃/₂方案实现了94%的双量子比特门保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索中性原子Rydberg激发实现双量子比特操作的不同方案，比较两种中间能级（5P₁/₂与6P₃/₂）在实验实现中的优劣，寻找提升量子操作保真度的关键因素。

Method: 搭建两种实验方案：方案一利用5P₁/₂中间能级，对目标原子局部施加第二阶Rydberg光束；方案二利用6P₃/₂能级，将待纠缠原子移至独立区域，两束Rydberg激光均通过该区域。使用自研Julia包进行数值建模，对比两种方案的空间构型与能级选择对保真度的影响。

Result: 数值模拟显示空间构型对量子操作保真度的影响大于中间能级的选择。实验实现了基于6P₃/₂能级的方案，双量子比特门保真度达到94%。

Conclusion: 两种Rydberg激发方案均可用于中性原子双量子比特操作；空间构型是影响保真度的主导因素而非中间能级选择；基于6P₃/₂能级的方案具备实验可行性，可达到较高保真度。

Abstract: This work presents an experimental setup for implementing two-qubit operations on neutral atoms ($^{87}$Rb) with the possibility of using two different Rydberg excitation schemes. One of them uses 5P$_{1/2}$ as the intermediate level and applies the second-stage beam locally to the addressed atoms. The second scheme uses the 6P$_{3/2}$ level; in this scheme, the particles to be entangled are moved to a separate zone through which both Rydberg beams pass. The advantages and limitations of both schemes are analyzed. Based on numerical modeling performed with a Julia package developed by the authors, it is demonstrated that the spatial configuration has a greater effect on quantum-operation fidelity than the choice of intermediate level. An experimental implementation of the scheme using the 6P$_{3/2}$ level is demonstrated, making it possible to achieve a two-qubit operation fidelity of 94%.

</details>


### [121] [All about quantum error correction: distillation, mitigation, self-correction and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14034)
*D. -S. Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了蒸馏、错误缓解、动力学去耦等量子错误管理技术都是量子纠错最一般框架的特例，通过将量子纠错扩展到状态自适应、信道自适应和多阶段编码场景实现统一视角，并提出了自纠正量子存储器模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子错误管理技术（蒸馏、错误缓解、动力学去耦等）看似相互独立，缺乏统一的理论基础来阐明它们之间的内在联系。

Method: 将量子纠错框架扩展至状态自适应和信道自适应设置，并引入多阶段编码场景，从而将多种错误管理技术纳入统一框架。

Result: 证明了多种量子错误管理技术是量子纠错一般框架的特例，提出自纠正量子存储器模型，并通过显式构造展示了统一视角如何指导可靠量子信息系统设计。

Conclusion: 统一框架厘清了各类量子错误管理技术之间的关系，为设计可靠的量子信息系统提供了指导性视角。

Abstract: In this work, it is shown that many quantum error-manipulating techniques, such as distillation, error mitigation, and dynamical decoupling, are special cases of the most general framework for quantum error correction. This unifying perspective is achieved by extending quantum error correction to include state-adaptive and channel-adaptive settings, as well as multi-stage coding scenarios. Based on this insight, a model of self-correcting quantum memory is also proposed. This work clarifies the relationship among these techniques and illustrates, through explicit constructions, how the unified perspective can guide the design of reliable quantum information systems.

</details>


### [122] [Link-Free Multi-Node Timing Synchronization for Scalable Quantum Networking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14077)
*Jacob E. Humberd,Mohmad Junaid Ul Haq,Angel Fraire Estrada,Ike Deitch,Tian Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了利用独立微型铷原子钟结合计算后处理，在无需专用定时分发基础设施（如White Rabbit网络）的情况下，实现城域级电信光纤网络中三个地理分离量子网络节点的链路无关同步，并在8小时连续运行中取得接近White Rabbit的定时性能，首次观测到无专用定时链路的城域量子干涉现象（Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉可见度>70%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式量子网络依赖White Rabbit等专用定时分发基础设施，这些架构限制了拓扑灵活性、可扩展性以及在自由空间和卫星环境中的部署能力。

Method: 采用独立运行的微型铷原子钟进行各节点本地定时，通过计算后处理（漂移校正）实现节点间同步，并在城域电信光纤网络上对三个地理分离节点进行实验验证。

Result: 原子钟同步方案在漂移校正后定时性能接近White Rabbit基准，在连续8小时运行中保持稳定；跨节点Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉可见度超过70%，与使用White Rabbit定时链路的结果在统计上等效。

Conclusion: 基于原子钟的同步方案是一种可扩展、拓扑无关的定时分发替代方案，为缺乏专用定时链路的陆基、空基和天基量子网络提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Precise timing synchronization is essential for distributed quantum networking, enabling entanglement distribution, quantum teleportation, and entanglement swapping across remote nodes. Existing synchronization architectures rely on dedicated timing-distribution infrastructure, most notably White Rabbit networks, which constrain topology, scalability, and deployment in free-space and satellite environments. Here we demonstrate link-free synchronization of quantum network nodes using independently operating miniature rubidium atomic clocks and computational post-processing. We validate the approach on a deployed metropolitan-scale telecom fiber network spanning three geographically separated nodes. Following drift correction, atomic-clock-based synchronization achieves timing performance approaching that of a White Rabbit benchmark and remains stable over continuous 8-hour operation. As a stringent test of quantum-network functionality, we observe Hong-Ou-Mandel interference across spatially separated nodes with visibility exceeding 70%, statistically equivalent to that obtained using dedicated White Rabbit timing links. To the best of our knowledge, this represents the first observation of quantum interference across a deployed metropolitan-scale telecom fiber network synchronized entirely without dedicated timing-transfer infrastructure. These results establish atomic-clock-based synchronization as a scalable, topology-independent alternative to conventional timing-distribution architectures and a practical pathway toward terrestrial, airborne, and space-based quantum networks where dedicated timing links are unavailable.

</details>


### [123] [OQMD: Single-Qubit Rotation Control Improves Low-CNOT Multiclass Quantum Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14088)
*Michael A. Magid,Melissa Zeynep Ertem,Jun Suzuki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出最优量子测量解码（OQMD），通过在测量前训练可训练的单量子比特读出层，在不增加CNOT门的情况下提升变分分类器性能。实验表明，0-CNOT配置可达83.33%准确率，9-CNOT配置可达96%，超越18-CNOT基线（56.67%）。最佳精度并非总出现在最大电路模板上，复杂度不能仅由CNOT数量概括。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期变分分类器因双量子比特门（CNOT）引入大量误差和延迟，但实践中常默认增加纠缠深度是提升精度的唯一途径。本文旨在探索不增加CNOT门的情况下，通过优化量子测量解码（即训练读出层）来提升分类性能，从而规避双量子比特门的开销。

Method: 提出最优量子测量解码（OQMD），在测量前引入可训练的单量子比特旋转层（如三重单量子比特旋转），与变分电路联合训练，但不增加CNOT门。实验在Iris数据集上，采用30点分层测试分割，构建0到18的CNOT深度系列，对比有无OQMD的配置，并以最佳观测分数和Mann-Whitney U检验进行非参数统计评估。

Result: 最佳0-CNOT配置（带OQMD）达83.33%准确率；9-CNOT配置（带OQMD）达96%，显著优于最佳18-CNOT对照组（56.67%）和最佳18-CNOT+OQMD配置（66.67%）。六点CNOT深度序列显示最高原始分数不一定出现在最大模板处。OQMD在不同架构上表现出统计一致但幅度依赖的增益：小电路上峰值提升大，复杂18-CNOT电路上平均偏移较小（p≈0.03），并非普遍的大规模实用效果。

Conclusion: OQMD能够在不增加CNOT门的情况下显著提升变分分类器精度，甚至在浅层电路上超越深层电路。模型复杂度不能仅由CNOT数量衡量，最佳精度并不必然对应最大电路模板。OQMD的增益在小电路上尤为显著，在复杂电路上虽统计显著但实际效果有限，表明其并非“普适性”的大幅改进手段。

Abstract: Near-term variational classifiers incur substantial error and latency from two-qubit gates, yet practitioners often assume that additional entangling depth is the default route to higher accuracy. This work studies Optimal Quantum Measurement Decoding (OQMD): optimizing how quantum outcomes are mapped to classical labels by training a readout layer before measurement, jointly with the variational circuit, without adding CNOTs. Experiments use trainable triple single-qubit rotations as one concrete, hardware-native realization of OQMD; other single-qubit parametrizations fit the same classical outer loop. On the Iris benchmark with a 30-point stratified test split, the best observed 0-CNOT configuration with OQMD reaches 83.33\% accuracy, with a 96\% at 9 CNOTs, exceeding the best 18-CNOT controls (56.67\%) and the best 18-CNOT configuration with OQMD (66.67\%) under a common protocol. A six-point CNOT-depth series from 0 to 18 (fixed optimizer, iteration budget, random-seed count, and ZXZ readout) shows that the highest raw scores need not occur at the largest template, so aggregate complexity is not summarized by CNOT count alone. Because run-level accuracies are discrete and non-Gaussian, we emphasize best-observed scores and, where a global comparison of pooled runs is required, Mann--Whitney $U$ tests rather than parametric tests on means. Across architectures, OQMD shows statistically consistent but magnitude-dependent gains: large peak lifts on minimal circuits coexist with a small pooled mean shift on complex 18-CNOT runs ($p\approx 0.03$) that is not ``universal'' in the sense of uniformly large practical effects.%

</details>


### [124] [Collision models for open quantum systems coupled to finite environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14163)
*Gyaneswar Bhoi,Anil Shaji*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究系统量子比特与同一环境量子比特重复碰撞的模型，发现在环境部分重置下，系统动力学在马尔可夫与非马尔可夫之间连续过渡，并由单一无量纲弛豫参数控制；通过精确解析求解和两种非马尔可夫度量，识别出三种不同动力学区域（CP-可分的马尔可夫、CP-不可分但P-可分的、以及非P-可分的非马尔可夫），并数值刻画了均匀与各向异性环境弛豫的结构差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究重复碰撞模型中系统-环境关联的完全抑制条件，以及部分环境重置如何导致马尔可夫性与非马尔可夫性之间的连续过渡，从而理解开放量子系统中的记忆效应演化机制。

Method: 采用重复碰撞模型（系统与环境量子比特反复作用，环境受CPTP映射演化），针对共振激子交换相互作用，推导了广义退极化通道和广义振幅阻尼通道下的Bloch矢量精确闭式解；利用Breuer-Laine-Piilo非马尔可夫度量与Choi矩阵CP-可分性判据对动力学进行区域划分。

Result: 完全抑制系统-环境关联需完全环境重置（恢复半群动力学）；部分重置则在CP-可分马尔可夫、CP-不可分但P-可分、以及非P-可分非马尔可夫三个区域间连续过渡。各向异性环境弛豫与均匀弛豫具有不同的区域边界结构。

Conclusion: 单一无量纲弛豫参数即可刻画从马尔可夫到非马尔可夫动力学的连续转变，为理解环境记忆效应的调控提供了简洁的理论框架。

Abstract: We study a system qubit repeatedly interacting with the same environmental qubit, with a reservoir acting on the environment between collisions via a completely positive, trace-preserving map. We show that complete suppression of system--environment correlations uniquely requires a full environmental reset, recovering a semi group dynamics with a time-independent Gorini--Kossakowski--Sudarshan--Lindblad generator, whereas a partial reset yields a continuous transition between Markovian and non-Markovian regimes governed by a single dimensionless relaxation parameter. For a resonant excitation-exchange interaction, we obtain exact closed-form expressions for the Bloch-vector dynamics for both a generalized depolarizing channel and a generalized amplitude-damping channel acting as the reservoir-induced map. Using the Breuer--Laine--Piilo measure and a Choi-matrix CP-divisibility witness, we identify three distinct dynamical regimes across the parameter space: CP-divisible Markovian dynamics, CP-indivisible but P-divisible dynamics, and non-P-divisible non-Markovian dynamics. The boundaries between these regimes, and the structural differences between uniform and anisotropic environmental relaxation, are characterized numerically.

</details>


### [125] [Dynamically frozen long-distance entanglement via non-Hermitian PT-symmetric systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14177)
*Sejal Ahuja,Keshav Das Agarwal,Aditi Sen De*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在分布式量子网络中，有效PT对称非厄米自旋-1/2体系统如何通过弱耦合两个量子链路，在非厄米SSH模型框架下动态生成并冻结长距离纠缠。研究发现，在虚数交错磁场驱动的非厄米XX模型中，系统在奇异点附近可从不分离初态演化出近单位纠缠，且纠缠冻结现象在厄米系统中不可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非厄米性与PT对称性在分布式量子网络中促进长距离纠缠生成的潜力，克服厄米系统中纠缠生成的局限性。

Method: 结合解析与数值方法，分析有效非厄米近邻SSH模型，研究虚数交错磁场下的量子XX体系统在奇异点附近的动力学纠缠生成，并优化时间与系统参数。

Result: 在弱虚数磁场和弱体-链耦合条件下，系统在奇异点附近自发演化为高度纠缠的链路态，时间平均纠缠接近单位值；非厄米动力学在奇异点附近表现出纠缠冻结行为，且长程相互作用模型可获得更高纠缠。

Conclusion: 工程化非厄米动力学可用于实现量子网络中鲁棒且冻结的纠缠链路，为非厄米量子信息处理提供新途径。

Abstract: In distributed quantum networks, interacting spin systems can mediate the generation of highly entangled links between distant nodes. We investigate the role of effective parity-time (PT)-symmetric non-Hermitian spin-1/2 bulks weakly coupled to two quantum links, obtained due to the environmental interactions affecting both the bulk and the links. Focusing on effective non-Hermitian nearest-neighbor (NN) Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) models, we analyze how non-Hermiticity influences the dynamical formation of long-distance entanglement (LDE). For a paradigmatic model consisting of a quantum XX bulk subjected to imaginary staggered magnetic fields, we analytically determine the exceptional points arising from the resulting bulk-mediated interactions between the links. Combining analytical and numerical methods, we demonstrate that an initially fully separable state can dynamically evolve into highly entangled link states near these exceptional points in the broken regime. Further, after optimizing over time and system parameters, near-unit time-averaged entanglement between the links emerges under weak imaginary magnetic fields and bulk-link couplings, which cannot be attained in the corresponding Hermitian systems. Moreover, the non-Hermitian dynamics exhibit a freezing of high entanglement in the vicinity of exceptional points, a feature absent in Hermitian counterparts. We also identify regimes of long-range interaction strengths that yield a higher time-averaged entanglement than the corresponding NN models. Furthermore, we establish that LDE persists in the stationary regime, highlighting the promise of engineered non-Hermitian dynamics for realizing robust and frozen entangled links in quantum networks.

</details>


### [126] [Simultaneous Estimation of Partial-Transpose Moments with Active Memory Independent of the Moment Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14204)
*Junxiang Huang,Xiaoyang Wang,Xiao Yuan,Yukun Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在主动量子内存约束下，从独立副本中同时估计未知二分n量子比特态的部分转置矩，提出了最多使用2n+1个活动量子比特的序贯量子比特重用实现方案，总副本复杂度为O(K log K/ε²)，并给出了两个反向界限，证明该复杂度在log因子意义下最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子信息论中，部分转置矩层次结构（partial-transpose moment hierarchy）是检测量子纠缠的重要工具，但如何高效地同时估计多个部分转置矩，特别是在主动量子内存（活跃量子比特数）受限的情况下，尚缺乏系统性的研究。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出了一种序贯量子比特重用（sequential qubit-reuse）的部分转置置换实现方案，仅需2n+1个活跃量子比特（与K无关），可同时估计p_2,...,p_K至均匀加性误差ε。同时通过构造显式的等谱两量子比特NPT族以及最坏情况分析，证明了两个下界结果。

Result: 1) 上界：总副本复杂度为O(K log K/ε²)；2) 下界：任何均匀准确的同步估计器在最坏情况下需要Ω(K/ε²)个副本；3) 在显式的等谱两量子比特NPT族上，该下界同样成立，表明普通矩为常数而部分转置矩变化时仍需相同规模。

Conclusion: 这些结果刻画了部分转置矩层次结构的副本复杂度至多相差一个对数因子，并将非线性泛函的同时估计从普通态幂拓展到部分转置谱数据，且主动量子内存与目标矩阶数无关。

Abstract: We study the simultaneous estimation of partial-transpose moments $p_j(ρ_{AB})=\mathrm{Tr}[(ρ_{AB}^{T_B})^j]$, $j=2,\ldots,K$, of an unknown bipartite $n$-qubit state from independent copies under an explicit active-memory constraint. We give a sequential qubit-reuse realization of the partial-transpose permutation that uses at most $2n+1$ active qubits, independent of $K$, and estimates all moments $p_2,\ldots,p_K$ to uniform additive error $ε$ with total copy complexity $O(K\log K/ε^2)$. We also prove two converse bounds. First, any uniformly accurate simultaneous estimator requires $Ω(K/ε^2)$ copies in the worst case. Second, the same scaling holds on an explicit isospectral two-qubit negative-partial-transpose (NPT) family whose ordinary moments are constant while the partial-transpose moments vary. These results characterize the copy complexity of the partial-transpose moment hierarchy up to a logarithmic factor and extend simultaneous nonlinear-functional estimation from ordinary state powers to partial-transpose spectral data under active quantum memory independent of the target moment order.

</details>


### [127] [Beyond the Unruh vacuum: multi-time correlations in black hole collapse and evaporation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13383)
*Konstantinos Xenos,Charis Anastopoulos,Andreas F. Terzis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明黑洞蒸发过程中，多时间量子场关联可以携带塌缩前物质的信息，尽管霍金辐射是热态的，但多时间关联中包含了依赖于初始态的贡献，这表明仅基于单时间观测量的信息悖论表述是不完整的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞信息佯谬源于霍金辐射的热性质，传统上认为热辐射会抹去塌缩物质的信息。但热性只约束单时间观测量，多时间量子关联的结构尚未被充分探索，本文旨在探究多时间关联是否能承载信息。

Method: 使用二维引力塌缩和蒸发模型，分析晚期多时间关联是否完全由Unruh真空复现，检查其对塌缩前态参数的依赖关系。

Result: 晚期多时间关联并非完全由Unruh真空决定，而是包含一个显式依赖于塌缩前态参数的贡献，尽管渐近辐射具有热特征。

Conclusion: 可测量的多时间关联是霍金辐射中信息的载体，仅基于单时间观测量的黑洞信息悖论表述是不完备的。

Abstract: The black hole information paradox originates from the thermal character of Hawking radiation, which appears to erase information about the collapsing matter. However, thermality constrains only observables defined at a single time and leaves the structure of temporal quantum correlations largely unexplored. Here we show that multi-time quantum-field correlations provide a concrete mechanism for the survival of pre-collapse information in black hole evaporation. Using a two-dimensional model of gravitational collapse and evaporation, we demonstrate that late-time multi-time correlations are not fully reproduced by the Unruh vacuum. In particular, they contain a contribution that depends explicitly on parameters characterizing the pre-collapse state, despite the thermal character of the asymptotic radiation. Our results identify measurable multi-time correlations as carriers of information in Hawking radiation and suggest that formulations of the black hole information paradox based solely on single-time observables are incomplete.

</details>


### [128] [Fourier analysis of quantum neural network with non-linear data embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14206)
*Haiyue Kang,Martin Sevior,Muhammad Usman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对振幅编码变分量子电路（VQC）建立了傅里叶分析框架，研究了非线性数据嵌入下的表达性，推导了含噪与无噪环境下傅里叶系数的均值与方差标度，并通过数值模拟验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有傅里叶分析仅适用于无噪声环境下的角度嵌入VQC，而振幅编码作为紧凑的自然编码方案，其傅里叶性质尚未被系统研究，尤其在含噪场景下的表达性与可训练性缺乏理论保证。

Method: 1) 分析振幅编码中输入特征域的微妙差异对零频傅里叶系数表达性的影响；2) 假设参数空间生成酉算子集合至少构成关于酉群的2-design，利用Weingarten微积分推导傅里叶系数的均值与方差；3) 引入带幺正Kraus算子的噪声信道，分析噪声对方差的影响；4) 通过无噪与含噪数值模拟验证理论结果。

Result: 傅里叶系数均值集中在零，方差随多维频率幅值呈指数级衰减；含噪环境下方差进一步被因子$(\sum_k p_k^2)^Q < 1$抑制；数值模拟验证了理论，包括非整数频率目标函数的分解场景。

Conclusion: 建立了振幅编码VQC的严格傅里叶框架，提供了频率域表达性与可训练性的理论保证，并为含噪量子设备上的实际部署提供了仿真基础。

Abstract: Fourier analysis has become a crucial tool for understanding the expressivity of Variational Quantum Circuit (VQC) models, as well as an important indicator of barren plateaus (BP). While existing literature has only studied angle-embedded VQCs in a noiseless environment, here we develop the Fourier analysis of VQCs with non-linear data embedding, with particular focus on amplitude embedding, which provides a naturally compact encoding scheme. We first investigate a subtle difference in the domain of input features within amplitude embedding that leads to a distinct expressivity of the zero-frequency Fourier coefficient. By assuming that the ensemble of unitaries generated from the parameter space forms at least a 2-design with respect to the unitary group, we derive, via Weingarten calculus, that the mean of the Fourier coefficients is concentrated at zero, and the variance scales at an exponentially decaying order with respect to the multi-dimensional frequency magnitude. When a noise channel with unitary Kraus operators and probabilities $\{p_k\}$ is taken into account, the variance is further suppressed by a factor $\left(\sum_k p_k^2\right)^{Q}<1$, where $Q$ is the number of channel instances applied. Furthermore, we demonstrate and validate the analytical results through simulations, both noiseless and noisy, including a case where target functions are decomposed into non-integer frequencies, highlighting the practical utility of the approach. Our results establish a rigorous Fourier framework for amplitude-encoded VQCs, offering both theoretical guarantees on expressivity, hence trainability scaling in the frequency domain, as well as practical simulations for deployment on noisy quantum devices.

</details>


### [129] [Efficient Simulation of Szegedy Quantum Walk Formulations and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14226)
*Sergio A. Ortega,Daniel K. Park*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Szegedy量子行走的高效经典模拟方法，避免显式构造完整酉演化算子，支持更广泛的行走形式，并扩展到基于相位估计算法，实现了稠密图O(N²)和稀疏图O(边数)的最优复杂度，开发了Python包SQWLib并通过量子模拟退火和量子搜索验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Szegedy量子行走的经典模拟方法局限于特定的行走形式，缺乏统一高效的模拟框架，难以支持更广泛的行走形式及基于相位估计的算法，需要一种更通用且高效的经典模拟方法。

Method: 基于基本的更新算子和反射算子构建统一框架，避免显式构造完整的酉演化算子；将方法扩展到基于相位估计算法，并针对大规模稀疏图进行优化；在Python包SQWLib中实现该框架。

Result: 稠密图（N个节点）下达到最优O(N²)复杂度；稀疏图下计算成本与边数呈线性关系（通常为O(N)）；通过量子模拟退火和量子搜索算法的仿真验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为超越纯理论分析、数值研究基于Szegedy行走的算法提供了实用工具，具有通用性和高效性。

Abstract: Quantum walks provide a versatile framework for quantum algorithms across a wide range of applications. We develop efficient classical simulation methods for Szegedy quantum walks that avoid explicit construction of the full unitary evolution operator. Unlike previous approaches restricted to a particular walk formulation, our framework is built from fundamental update and reflection operators, enabling the simulation of a broader class of Szegedy walk formulations. We further extend these methods to phase-estimation-based algorithms coupled to the walk, including implementations suitable for large sparse graphs. The resulting methods achieve optimal $O(N^2)$ complexity for dense graphs with $N$ nodes. For sparse graphs, the computational cost scales linearly with the number of edges, which is $O(N)$ in many cases. We implement the framework in the Python package SQWLib and illustrate its capabilities through simulations of representative algorithms, including quantum simulated annealing and quantum search on graphs. These results provide a practical tool for studying Szegedy-walk-based algorithms numerically beyond purely analytical treatments.

</details>


### [130] [Quantum codes and optimal pure quantum $(r,δ)$-LRCs via the MP construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14253)
*Meng Cao,Kun Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用τ-最优定义（τ-OD）矩阵构造MP码，进而构造新的量子码和量子(r,δ)-局部修复码（LRCs）。主要成果包括：建立了任意特征有限域上可逆自伴矩阵的统一τ-单项分解定理（推广了此前仅适用于奇特征的结果）；证明了在任意特征下的F_{q^2}上τ-OD矩阵的存在性，并给出了特征2下的若干新无穷族；构造了多个参数灵活的无穷族量子码，其中包括222个破纪录的量子码（超越Grassl数据库）。此外，提出了两种通过MP码构造最优纯量子(r,δ)-LRCs的有效方案，构建了四个参数灵活的无穷族，并发现30个同时为最优纯量子(r,δ)-LRCs和最佳/破纪录量子码的例子——这一双重性质此前未见报道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广τ-OD矩阵构造量子码的理论框架（从奇特征到任意特征），并利用MP码构造具有局部修复能力的最优量子LRCs，同时追求量子码参数的最优性和记录突破。

Method: 采用τ-OD（τ-最优定义）矩阵作为MP码的定义矩阵，建立统一的τ-单项分解定理（覆盖任意特征有限域），基于此证明τ-OD矩阵在F_{q^2}上的存在性并构造新无穷族；通过MP码框架设计两种方案构造最优纯量子(r,δ)-LRCs。

Result: 建立了任意特征下自伴矩阵的τ-单项分解定理；证明了任意特征F_{q^2}上τ-OD矩阵的存在性，得到特征2下的新无穷族；构造222个破纪录量子码；构造四个新无穷族的最优纯量子(r,δ)-LRCs；发现30个同时满足最优LRC和最佳/破纪录量子码的实例。

Conclusion: τ-OD矩阵框架可统一处理任意特征有限域，为量子码和量子LRCs的构造提供了有力工具；首次发现量子码可同时具备最优局部修复性和全局最优参数（破纪录），这是文献中未曾报道的新现象。

Abstract: In this paper, we employ MP codes whose defining matrices are $τ$-optimal defining ($τ$-OD) matrices to construct new quantum codes and quantum $(r,δ)$-LRCs. Specifically, we report the following results: We establish a unified $τ$-monomial decomposition theorem for invertible self-adjoint matrices over finite fields of arbitrary characteristic, which generalizes the result in "Quantum codes using the $τ$-OD MP construction" where the characteristic was required to be odd. Based on this theorem, we prove the existence of $τ$-OD matrices over $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ for any characteristic and demonstrate that there exist several new infinite families of $τ$-OD matrices over $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ of characteristic $2$. As an application of MP codes involving $τ$-OD matrices, we construct several infinite families of quantum codes with flexible parameters. Within this framework, we present $222$ record-breaking quantum codes that surpass the best-known records maintained in Grassl's database.
  We propose two effective schemes for constructing optimal pure quantum $(r,δ)$-LRCs via MP codes. Accordingly, we construct four new infinite families of optimal pure quantum $(r,δ)$-LRCs with flexible parameters. Notably, we report an interesting phenomenon by exhibiting $30$ optimal pure quantum $(r,δ)$-LRCs derived from our framework; that is, there exist quantum codes that are not only optimal pure quantum $(r,δ)$-LRCs but also, according to Grassl's database, best-known, optimal, or record-breaking quantum codes. To the best of our knowledge, the new discovery that quantum codes are simultaneously optimal pure quantum $(r,δ)$-LRCs and record-breaking quantum codes has not been previously reported in the literature.

</details>


### [131] [Dose-efficient Quantum Phase Estimation in Lossy Optical Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14254)
*Qilin Yu,Ben Wang,Kaimin Zheng,Minghao Mi,Hui Li,Lijian Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了在存在光子损耗的情况下，采用顺序策略实现剂量受限的量子相位估计。通过量子Fisher信息（QFI）每剂量评估，发现带控制的顺序策略超越了经典极限和并行策略，接近量子极限，为有损环境下的高效量子计量学提供了实用途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物成像等应用中，光剂量需严格受限以避免对光敏样本造成损害。传统量子增强干涉测量虽能提高精度，但光子损耗（如样本吸收）会严重削弱量子优势。因此，亟需探索在损耗环境下仍能保持高精度、高剂量效率的相位估计方法。

Method: 实验采用顺序策略（含控制增强和无控制两种变体）进行量子相位估计，在存在光子损耗的条件下，通过量子Fisher信息（QFI）每剂量作为性能指标，与经典极限和使用不平衡N00N态的并行策略进行对比。

Result: 两种顺序策略均超越经典极限，且优于使用不平衡N00N态的并行策略。其中，控制增强的顺序策略获得了最高的QFI每剂量，接近量子极限。

Conclusion: 顺序策略，尤其是控制增强版本，在有损和资源受限的场景下（如生物成像）具有显著优势，是迈向实用化、高效量子计量学的重要一步。

Abstract: Optical interferometry is a cornerstone technique for precise phase measurements across various fields. In many applications, for example, biological imaging, it often necessitates stringent limits on light intensity to prevent adverse effects on light-sensitive samples, a condition known as dose-limited regimes. Maximizing the precision per dose is therefore crucial. In quantum metrology, quantum correlations enable high precision in phase estimation while adhering to dose constraints. Nevertheless, photon loss, including absorption by a sample, substantially diminishes the benefits of quantum enhancement in interferometry. In this work, we experimentally investigate a dose-efficient approach to quantum phase estimation using sequential strategies in the presence of loss. Performance of sequential strategies with and without control is evaluated through quantum Fisher information (QFI) per dose. Experimental results show that both sequential strategies exceed the classical limit and outperform the parallel strategy using unbalanced N00N states. Notably, the control-enhanced sequential strategy attains superior QFI per dose, approaching the quantum limit. These results highlight the promise of sequential strategy for imaging and sensing in resource-constrained scenarios, marking a significant step toward practical and efficient quantum metrology in lossy environments.

</details>


### [132] [A new class of degenerate solutions to the massless Dirac equation and their potential applications in optical memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14256)
*Georgios N. Tsigaridas,Aristides I. Kechriniotis,Christos A. Tsonos,Konstantinos K. Delibasis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的无质量狄拉克方程退化解，对应多种电磁4势和场，包括零场和圆极化电磁波。这些解的粒子自旋与电磁波电场磁场同步旋转，可用于基于支持无质量狄拉克费米子的材料（如石墨烯）的光学存储器开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无质量狄拉克方程的新解，特别是那些自旋与电磁场同步旋转的解，以推动基于石墨烯等材料的光学存储器发展。

Method: 提出了一类新的退化解，应用于无质量狄拉克方程，涵盖多种电磁4势和场（包括零场和圆极化电磁波）。

Result: 发现了粒子自旋与电磁波电场磁场同步旋转的特性。

Conclusion: 这些解可用于开发基于支持无质量狄拉克费米子的材料（如石墨烯）的光学存储器。

Abstract: In this article, we present a novel class of degenerate solutions to the massless Dirac equation, corresponding to a wide variety of electromagnetic 4-potentials and fields, including both zero field and circularly polarized electromagnetic waves. An interesting property of these solutions is that the spin of the particles rotates in synchronization with the electric and magnetic fields of the electromagnetic waves. These results could be utilized for the development of optical memories based on materials supporting massless Dirac fermions, such as graphene.

</details>


### [133] [Quantum-Classical Hierarchical Equations of Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14363)
*Amartya Bose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子-经典分层运动方程（QC-HEOM）方法，用于模拟非马尔可夫开放量子系统。该方法通过将热涨落分离到参考经典轨迹中，仅需对残余量子记忆进行分层处理，从而避免了传统HEOM中对Matsubara或Padé展开的依赖，显著降低了低温下的辅助对象数量，并能自然扩展到非简谐环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分层运动方程（HEOM）在低温下需要大量Matsubara或Padé展开项，计算成本高昂；且局限于简谐浴环境，无法处理非简谐或分子环境。因此需要一种更高效、更通用的开放量子系统模拟方法。

Method: QC-HEOM将量子-经典路径积分形式中的系综平均经典路径参考与辅助量子影响泛函的分层相结合。通过Wigner轨迹的系综平均引入热涨落，分层仅需表示浴响应函数虚部对应的残余量子记忆。该方法无需热核的Matsubara或Padé展开，层级大小仅弱依赖于温度，且可通过外部生成的轨迹扩展到非简谐环境。

Result: 在简谐浴中证明了QC-HEOM的精确性；对非对称自旋-玻色子模型和七位点Fenna-Matthews-Olson复合物的应用表明，QC-HEOM在显著减少辅助对象数量的同时，准确复现了准绝热路径积分和传统HEOM的基准结果，尤其在低温下优势明显。

Conclusion: QC-HEOM为量子-经典开放系统动力学中处理残余量子记忆提供了一种高效框架。通过Wigner轨迹分离热涨落与残余量子记忆的思想，为传统HEOM无法处理的复杂非简谐环境的分层模拟开辟了近似路径。

Abstract: We develop a quantum-classical hierarchical equations of motion (QC-HEOM) approach for simulating non-Markovian open quantum systems. The method combines the ensemble-averaged classical path reference of the quantum-classical path integral formalism with a hierarchy of auxiliary quantum influence functionals. By incorporating thermal fluctuations through an ensemble average over reference trajectories, the hierarchy is required to represent only the residual quantum memory associated with the imaginary part of the bath response function. Consequently, unlike conventional hierarchical equations of motion, QC-HEOM does not require Matsubara or Padé expansions of the thermal kernel and exhibits only weak temperature dependence of the hierarchy size. Furthermore, because thermal fluctuations are supplied through reference classical trajectories, the framework naturally extends beyond harmonic baths and enables the incorporation of anharmonic and molecular environments through externally generated trajectories. We derive the formalism and demonstrate its exactness for a harmonic bath. Applications to an asymmetric spin-boson model and the seven-site Fenna--Matthews--Olson complex illustrate the accuracy of QC-HEOM. It reproduces benchmark quasi-adiabatic path integral and hierarchical equations of motion results while requiring substantially fewer auxiliary objects, particularly at low temperatures. These results establish QC-HEOM as an efficient framework for treating residual quantum memory in quantum-classical descriptions of open-system dynamics. The separation of thermal fluctuations from residual quantum memory through the use of Wigner trajectories provides an approximate route toward hierarchical treatments of complex anharmonic environments that are inaccessible to conventional HEOM approaches.

</details>


### [134] [Certification of the genuine resolution of photon number resolving detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14365)
*Jef Pauwels,Towsif Taher,Roope Uola,Boris Korzh,Nicolas Brunner,Pavel Sekatski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量化光子数分辨探测器真正分辨能力的操作框架，并开发了基于相干态探针的实用认证协议，在28像素超导纳米线单光子探测器上实现了四光子分辨的认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏实用的度量标准来认证光子数分辨探测器在单次测量中真正能分辨多少光子，这阻碍了光量子技术的发展。

Method: 提出了一个操作框架来量化PNR探测器的真正分辨能力，并开发了基于相干态探针的实用可扩展认证协议。

Result: 将该方法应用于28像素PNR-SNSPD，认证了其真正的四输出分辨能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为PNR探测器提供了操作基准，填补了光量子器件表征中的关键空白，并突出了探测器效率对实现高真正分辨率的关键要求。

Abstract: Photon-number-resolving (PNR) detectors are essential components of photonic quantum technologies, yet thus far, no practical metric exists to certify how many photons they can genuinely resolve in a single measurement. Here we introduce an operational framework for quantifying the capability of a PNR detector to distinguish between different numbers of photons, i.e. its genuine resolution. In turn, we develop a practical and scalable protocol for certifying the genuine resolution of a detector, which is based on coherent state probes. We apply the method to a 28-pixel photon-number-resolving superconducting nanowire single-photon detector (PNR-SNSPD) and certify genuine four-outcome resolution. Our work highlights the critical requirements in terms of detector efficiency towards achieving high genuine resolution. This approach provides an operational benchmark for PNR detectors and fills a crucial gap in the characterization of photonic quantum devices.

</details>


### [135] [Quantum sensing through bosonic-fermionic Bell-state transitions in two-photon interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14408)
*Chahat Kaushik,Vimlesh Kumar,G. K. Samanta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于交换对称性控制的量子传感方案，通过将几何相位印刻到泵浦光并转移至偏振纠缠光子，实现Bell态在对称(玻色子型)和反对称(费米子型)之间的连续调控，从而在保持光子时空不可区分性的同时调制HOM干涉响应，并用于高分辨率双折射测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HOM传感方案需将样品插入干涉仪一臂，易受光学损耗、对准不稳定性和带宽相关的干涉轮廓畸变影响，亟需一种更鲁棒的量子传感方法。

Method: 通过自发参量下转换(SPDC)产生偏振纠缠光子对，在经典泵浦光束上施加几何相位并转移至纠缠态，连续调控纠缠态的交换对称性，实现HOM干涉从聚束到反聚束的正弦平方相位依赖调制。

Result: 实现了约10^4 counts/s的符合计数调制；相位调制线宽固定为π/2，与光子带宽无关；利用置于泵浦光路上的双折射晶体，在宽温度范围内实现了约10^{-6}量级的热色散双折射分辨率。

Conclusion: 交换对称性可作为可控资源用于鲁棒量子传感和对称性工程化光子量子信息处理，突破了传统HOM传感对带宽的依赖和易受干扰的限制。

Abstract: Hong-Ou-Mandel (HOM) interference has become a central resource for quantum sensing and metrology owing to its sensitivity to temporal delay and photon indistinguishability. However, existing HOM-based sensing schemes generally rely on inserting a sample into one arm of the interferometer, making the measurement vulnerable to optical loss, alignment instability, and bandwidth-dependent distortion of the interference profile. Here, we demonstrate a symmetry-controlled quantum sensing scheme based on continuous transitions between symmetric (bosonic-like) and antisymmetric (fermionic-like) Bell states in two-photon interference. By imprinting a geometric phase onto the classical pump beam and transferring it to polarization-entangled photons generated via spontaneous parametric down-conversion, we coherently tune the exchange symmetry of the entangled state without altering the temporal or spectral indistinguishability of the photons. The HOM response evolves continuously from bunching to antibunching with a sine square phase dependence, producing a coincidence modulation of approximately 10 * 10^4 counts s^-1 counts/s. In contrast to conventional HOM sensing, the phase-modulation linewidth remains fixed at pi/2, independent of photon bandwidth. Using a birefringent crystal placed directly in the pump beam, we measure thermo-dispersive birefringence with a resolution of the order of 10^{-6} over a broad temperature range. Our results establish exchange symmetry as a controllable resource for robust quantum sensing and symmetry-engineered photonic quantum information processing.

</details>


### [136] [Jones-matrix analysis of phase accumulation in a linear-optical multi-pass interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14422)
*Byoung S. Ham*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Nature 450, 393 (2007)中提出的多通光子方案，通过琼斯矩阵形式化证明其超分辨率和超灵敏度的物理起源并非量子非经典资源，而是偏振态在庞加莱球上的相干旋转积累的几何相位，并通过经典波实验和Fisher信息分析验证了这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清Nature 450, 393 (2007)中多通光子方案观测到的超分辨率及其伴随的超灵敏度声明的物理起源，探究其是否真正依赖非经典量子资源。

Method: 采用严格的琼斯矩阵形式化分析HQMQ线性光学单元的往返演化，证明其等价于偏振空间中两次反射的乘积，形成有效旋转算符；开展经典波实验验证理论模型；利用Fisher信息分析标度行为以检验超灵敏度起源。

Result: HQMQ单元的往返演化等价于偏振空间中的两次反射，累积相位源于偏振态在庞加莱球上的相干旋转；经典波实验与琼斯矩阵解一致；Fisher信息分析揭示了超灵敏度的实际起源。

Conclusion: 该方案中观察到的超分辨率和超灵敏度并非源于非经典量子资源，而是源于相干偏振态旋转的几何相位积累，这一机制与最近发展的相干德布罗意波长框架实现超分辨率的原理相同。

Abstract: Quantum information science has traditionally relied on nonclassical resources, such as entangled photon pairs and squeezed states, to achieve measurement performance beyond classical limits. Here, we revisit the multi-pass photonic scheme reported in Nature 450, 393 (2007) to clarify the physical origin of the observed superresolution and the associated claim of supersensitivity. Using a rigorous Jones-matrix formalism, we show that the round-trip evolution of the HQMQ linear optics unit is equivalent to the product of two reflections in polarization space, resulting in an effective rotation operator. This equivalence reveals that the accumulated phase arises from coherent polarization-state rotation on the Poincare'e sphere. The resulting phase accumulation is interpreted geometrically as a progressive realignment of the polarization state during successive forward and backward propagations. To validate the theoretical model, a classical-wave implementation is experimentally conducted, analyzed, and compared with the corresponding Jones-matrix solution. Finally, the scaling behavior of the Fisher information is analyzed to examine the origin of the claimed supersensitivity. The results are further compared with a recently developed coherence de Broglie wavelength framework, which achieves identical superresolution through repeated coherent interactions in a cascaded interferometeric architecture.

</details>


### [137] [Spin counting via projection noise measurement of mesoscopic solid-state spin ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14437)
*Lorenzo Bechelli,Konstantin Herb,Laura Alicia Völker,Christian L. Degen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了在室温下直接测量氮空位(NV)色心自旋系综的量子投影噪声，通过偏振选择性光激发和自旋-电荷转换实现超过20%的ODMR对比度，成功对多达43个自旋的介观系综进行投影噪声测量和自旋计数，并展示了该协议在磁测量灵敏度上的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态自旋系综中的量子投影噪声测量因经典读出噪声占主导而面临挑战，此前仅在原子系统中实现投影噪声极限测量，缺乏对固态自旋系综的直接观测手段。

Method: 结合偏振选择性光激发与自旋-电荷转换技术，获得了单晶取向NV自旋缺陷超过20%的高ODMR对比度，从而在室温下直接观测介观NV自旋系综的量子投影噪声。

Result: 实现了对多达43个自旋的纳米级NV系综的投影噪声测量与自旋计数；该协议在磁测量应用中显著提升了灵敏度，且无需低温操作或高偏置磁场。

Conclusion: 该工作为固态自旋系综的量子投影噪声研究提供了实验基础，并为室温下高灵敏度量子传感应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Quantum projection noise is the fundamental noise source for the population measurement of spin ensembles. While projection-noise-limited measurements have been extensively studied in atomic systems, corresponding experiments on solid-state spin ensembles remain challenging due to dominant classical readout noise. Here, we report direct measurement of the quantum projection noise of mesoscopic ensembles of nitrogen-vacancy (NV) spin defects at room temperature. Our experiment is enabled by a high optically-detected magnetic resonance (ODMR) contrast of over 20% for a single crystallographic orientation of the defect spins, obtained by combining polarization-selective optical excitation with spin-to-charge conversion. We use our protocol to demonstrate projection noise measurements and spin counting from nanoscale NV ensembles of up to 43 spins. We further demonstrate that the protocol allows for significant gains in sensitivity for magnetometry applications without need for cryogenic operation or high bias magnetic fields.

</details>


### [138] [Dealing with locality in QAOA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14447)
*Mithilesh Kumar,Yusuf Tahir*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种传输增强型QAOA（transport-augmented QAOA），通过在混频器中添加优化的捷径耦合（scheduled XX+YY）来压缩交互图直径，从而克服浅层QAOA在稀疏、高直径MaxCut实例上的局部性瓶颈。实验表明，在二分图族（直径4）上，p=1时平均近似比从0.7378提升至0.9767；在随机树（直径10）上，p=2时从0.9226提升至0.9997。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅层QAOA在处理稀疏、高直径MaxCut实例时存在局部性瓶颈：在深度p下，局部可观测量仅依赖于电路交互图的有限邻域，导致性能受限。

Method: 保持MaxCut代价哈密顿量不变，在混频器中添加优化的、无权的捷径耦合（scheduled XX+YY）以压缩有效交互图直径；利用精确有限深度支持递归分析最优捷径放置与有界直径图增广的关系。

Result: 二分图族（直径4）：p=1时平均近似比从ma-QAOA的0.7378提升至0.9767（σ=0.0251，9种系统尺寸）；随机树（直径10）：p=2时从0.9226提升至0.9997（σ=0.0001）。性能在直径降低后变得与系统尺寸近似无关。

Conclusion: 传输增强型QAOA通过压缩交互图直径有效克服了局部性瓶颈，性能显著优于ma-QAOA，且对系统尺寸具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Shallow-depth QAOA on sparse, high-diameter MaxCut instances faces a locality bottleneck: at depth \(p\), local observables can depend only on a bounded neighborhood of the circuit interaction graph. We propose a transport-augmented QAOA that keeps the MaxCut cost Hamiltonian unchanged but enriches the mixer with optimized, unweighted shortcut couplings (scheduled \(XX+YY\)) to collapse the effective interaction-graph diameter. Using exact finite-depth support recursions, we relate optimal shortcut placement to bounded-diameter graph augmentation, and show in benchmarks that (unlike ma-QAOA) performance becomes effectively size-invariant once the diameter is reduced. For bipartite families (base diameter 4), reducing the interaction path to \(d=1\) raises the ensemble-averaged approximation ratio from 0.7378 (ma-QAOA) to 0.9767 at \(p=1\) (\(σ=0.0251\), nine system sizes); on random trees (base diameter 10), at \(p=2\) it improves from 0.9226 to 0.9997 (\(σ=0.0001\)).

</details>


### [139] [Optimal Decoding of Small Codes by Density Matrix Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14455)
*Anthony Benois,Pierre Cussenot,Grégoire Misguich,Nicolas Sangouard,Kiara Hansenne*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过对比最大似然解码基准，研究了电路级噪声下实际解码算法的精度。作者提出带严格边界的剪枝技术，能够处理更多轮次的症候提取，并将其应用于重复码和细胞自动机码的小规模实例。研究发现，标准解码器在重复码上保持接近最优，但在细胞自动机码上显著偏离最优，其中BP+OSD在实验相关噪声下性能恶化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际解码器依赖启发式近似，但难以量化其与最优解码之间的差距，因为这强烈依赖于所考虑的代码和噪声模型。本文旨在通过最大似然解码基准来量化这种差距。

Method: 通过完整记忆实验传播密度矩阵，为每个症候历史计算最优解码决策；引入带有严格边界的剪枝技术以扩展可处理的症候提取轮次数。

Result: 标准解码器在重复码上接近最优；但在细胞自动机码上显著偏离最优，BP+OSD在实验相关噪声下性能恶化；低物理错误率下仅少数症候历史对逻辑错误率有显著贡献。

Conclusion: 不同解码器在不同代码下的表现存在显著差异，需要针对具体代码和噪声模型评估解码策略；剪枝方法揭示了低噪声下症候历史重要性分布的高度偏斜性。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient decoding is a crucial component for achieving fault-tolerant quantum computing. Realistic circuit-level noise introduces temporal correlations and degeneracy, making optimal (maximum-likelihood) decoding computationally intractable in general. As a result, practical decoders rely on heuristic approximations, and it is generally difficult to quantify how suboptimal they are, as this strongly depends on the code and noise model considered. In this work, we study the accuracy of practical decoding algorithms under circuit-level noise by comparing them against a maximum likelihood decoding benchmark. Our approach propagates the density matrix through the full memory experiment and computes the optimal decoding decision for each syndrome history. We introduce pruning techniques with rigorous bounds, allowing us to access larger numbers of syndrome-extraction rounds. We apply this framework to small instances of the repetition code and a cellular automaton code, and benchmark minimum-weight perfect matching (MWPM), belief propagation with ordered statistics decoding (BP+OSD), Tesseract, and Planar decoders against optimal decoding. While standard decoders remain close to optimal for the repetition code, we find significant deviations for the cellular automaton code, with BP+OSD deteriorating already in experimentally relevant noise regimes. Moreover, the pruning method developed here highlights that, at low physical error rates, only a narrow fraction of syndrome histories contributes significantly to the logical error rate.

</details>


### [140] [QCI Connect: A Modular Full-Stack Quantum Computing Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14456)
*Eric Bertok,Hannes Busche,Florian Drinkler,David Emmanuel-Costa,Daniel Herr,Steffen Hien,Thomas Keitzl,Elisabeth Lobe,Alexandru Paler,Johannes Renkl,Martin Rymarz,Gary Schmiedinghoff,Peter K. Schuhmacher,Thomas Stehle,Benedikt Strobel,Hanna Tschakert,Adrian Vetter,Andre Waschk,Alexander Weinert,Lukas Windgätter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了QCI Connect及其SDK，一个硬件无关的全栈量子计算平台，采用模块化设计和开源接口定义，旨在通过云服务将量子计算硬件能力提供给领域专家，并促进社区驱动的应用生态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多种竞争量子计算架构并存的世界中，需要硬件无关的全栈平台，通过云服务将量子计算硬件的全部能力带给领域专家，同时促进社区驱动的应用生态系统发展。

Method: 提出了QCI Connect及其SDK作为全栈平台的参考架构，采用模块化设计和开源接口定义。

Result: 呈现了平台的总体设计、功能特性、核心接口以及经验教训，既面向平台用户也作为未来开发的参考指南。

Conclusion: QCI Connect为量子计算领域提供了一个硬件无关、模块化、开源的参考架构，有助于推动量子计算应用生态系统的构建。

Abstract: In a world of various competing quantum computing architectures, hardware-agnostic, full-stack platforms are necessary to bring the full power of quantum computing hardware to domain experts via the cloud. QCI Connect and its Software Development Kit provide a reference architecture for a full-stack platform with a modular design and open-source interface definitions, built to facilitate a community-driven application ecosystem. Here, we present its overall design and features, central interfaces, and lessons learned, both for users of the platform and as a reference guide for future developments.

</details>


### [141] [Sensitivity of polaron-molecule observables to MDR/GUP-like ultraviolet deformations at low energies via quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14479)
*Ezequiel Valero,Hugo Catala,Victor Ilisie,Germán Rodrigo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了多体杂质观测量对广义不确定性原理和修正色散关系类型的紫外形变具有增强的敏感性，通过构建保持红外区的形变极化子-分子哈密顿量，在量子计算平台上量化了这些形变对光谱和Ramsey观测量的影响，并在超导量子处理器上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索低能量子引力现象学在可及能标下的可观测效应，建立基于多体量子系统的灵敏度增强路径来检验紫外形变理论。

Method: 构建保持红外区的形变极化子-分子哈密顿量，在受控量子计算环境中模拟动力学过程，并在QRed超导量子处理器上进行实验验证。

Result: 在极化子-分子交叉区域附近，微小的紫外形变被强烈放大，导致准粒子性质和光谱响应出现实验可分辨的变化。

Conclusion: 建立了通往低能量子引力现象学的具体灵敏度路径，界定了有效描述的适用范围，并在超导量子处理器上获得实验验证。

Abstract: We show that impurity many-body observables can display enhanced sensitivity to ultraviolet deformations of generalized-uncertainty-principle and modified-dispersion-relation type at accessible energy scales. Using a deformed polaron-molecule Hamiltonian constructed to preserve the infrared sector, we quantify the impact of such deformations on spectral and Ramsey observables and implement the corresponding dynamics in a controlled quantum computing setting. We identify regimes near the polaron-molecule crossover where small ultraviolet deformations are strongly amplified, leading to experimentally resolvable changes in quasiparticle properties and spectral response. Our results establish a concrete sensitivity-based route to low-energy quantum-gravity phenomenology in a well-defined many-body platform and delimit the validity of the effective description. Furthermore, we report experimental validation on the QRed superconducting quantum processor (BSC-CNS).

</details>


### [142] [Quantum Horizon: An evaluation of quantum computing as a threat to Bitcoin and Ethereum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14484)
*Iosif M. Gershteyn,Jacob A. Alber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子计算对比特币和以太坊构成真实但有限且可缓解的威胁。Shor算法可破解ECDSA/BLS签名，而Grover算法对PoW挖矿威胁不大。集成预测显示约60%概率在2050年前出现密码相关量子计算机，但通过及时迁移可有效防御，核心约束在于治理而非技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清公众讨论中常混淆的两种量子算法对加密货币的实际影响，量化量子计算威胁的时间窗口与风险敞口，评估迁移可行性，指出治理才是关键瓶颈。

Method: 区分Shor算法和Grover算法的不同威胁场景；将硬件扩展、资源需求下降、容错准备滞后和专家调查纳入蒙特卡洛预测模型；分类统计各加密货币的量子暴露资产占比。

Result: 密码相关量子计算机的出现概率：2035年约1/6，2040年近30%，2050年约60%。比特币约600万枚量子暴露币中仅约230万枚不可迁移；以太坊50-65%的ETH位于可升级为后量子签名的密钥暴露账户。前20大加密货币均未实现完全后量子化。

Conclusion: 量子威胁真实存在但可通过迁移有效应对；及时迁移比乐观的2035年量子计算机更早完成；关键约束是治理而非技术；所有定量声明均有可复现模型支撑。

Abstract: Quantum computing poses a real, broad-based, but bounded and substantially mitigable threat to Bitcoin and Ethereum. We separate the two quantum algorithms that public discussion routinely conflates: Shor's algorithm breaks the elliptic-curve signatures (ECDSA over secp256k1, BLS over BLS12-381) that authorize spending, whereas Grover's algorithm does not meaningfully threaten proof-of-work mining, which is protected by a merely quadratic speedup, fault-tolerant per-operation costs, a square-root parallelization wall, and difficulty adjustment. Folding hardware scaling, the falling resource requirement, a fault-tolerance readiness lag, and expert surveys into a single Monte-Carlo forecast yields a wide, bimodal arrival distribution for a cryptographically relevant quantum computer: about a one-in-six chance by 2035, near 30% by 2040, and about 60% by 2050. Exposure is concentrated and mostly migratable: of Bitcoin's roughly six million quantum-exposed coins only about 2.3 million are irreducibly at risk, while 50 to 65% of Ether sits at key-revealed accounts that can adopt post-quantum signatures. A timely migration beats even an optimistic 2035 machine, so the binding constraint is governance, not technology. A survey of the top twenty cryptocurrencies finds none fully post-quantum. Reproducible models accompany every quantitative claim.

</details>


### [143] [Physics-Informed Variational Quantum Classifier for Phase Detection in Strongly Correlated Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14489)
*Hugo Catalá,Ezequiel Valero,Germán Rodrigo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理启发的变分量子分类器（VQC），通过Trotter化时间演化构建量子架构，用于检测费米极化子准粒子与分子束缚态之间的拓扑相变。该分类器在超导量子处理器上得到验证，具有线性门复杂度O(N)，能有效区分BEC和BCS区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联系统中量子相的表征对于量子传感器的部署至关重要，而传统机器学习方法缺乏可解释性且面临经典模拟的指数级内存墙问题。

Method: 采用物理启发的变分量子分类器（VQC），通过Trotter化有效哈密顿量的时间演化构建量子架构，使可学习参数对应可解释的物理量（演化时间和有效浴相互作用），并在QRed超导量子处理器上验证。

Result: VQC能高效发现最优干涉协议，最大化Ramsey条纹可见性以区分BEC和BCS区域。尽管存在硬件噪声和退相干，该分类器仍能保持拓扑相的相对排序，并且门复杂度为O(N)。

Conclusion: 物理启发的量子架构在噪声环境下仍能有效检测拓扑相变，且线性门复杂度确保了向多体区域的可扩展性，克服了经典模拟的指数级内存墙问题。

Abstract: The characterisation of quantum phases in strongly correlated systems is a crucial milestone for the deployment of quantum sensors. In this work, we present a Physics-Informed Variational Quantum Classifier (VQC) designed to detect the topological phase transition between the Fermi polaron quasiparticle and the molecular bound state. Unlike conventional Machine Learning approaches, our quantum architecture is constructed via the Trotterised time-evolution of an effective Hamiltonian, ensuring that the learnable parameters correspond to interpretable physical quantities. We show that the VQC efficiently discovers the optimal interferometric protocol, specifically the evolution time and effective bath interactions required to maximise the visibility of Ramsey fringes, thereby clearly distinguishing the Bose-Einstein Condensate (BEC) and Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) regimes. Furthermore, we report the validation of this classifier on the QRed superconducting quantum processor (BSC-CNS). Despite the intrinsic hardware noise and decoherence, the VQC preserves the relative ordering of the topological phases. We demonstrate that the physics-informed architecture achieves a linear gate complexity $\mathcal{O}(N)$, bypassing the exponential memory wall of classical simulation and ensuring scalability to many-body regimes.

</details>


### [144] [Quantum gates with parametrically driven multi-qubit couplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14522)
*Verena Feulner,Marjan Fani,Lukas Heunisch,Stephan Tasler,Michael J. Hartmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种通过中心可调耦合电路连接的四量子比特plaquette结构，实现了对角量子比特之间的√iSWAP门（50 ns, 99.9%保真度）以及三量子比特门（200 ns, 95%保真度），并分析了并行操作时的串扰影响（保真度降至99.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子处理器需要增强量子比特间的连接性以及精确控制相互作用和门操作，以提升量子计算能力。

Method: 采用中心可调耦合电路连接四个量子比特形成plaquette结构，通过数值和解析方法分析参量驱动过程，实现任意量子比特对（包括对角）的√iSWAP门以及三量子比特相互作用和门操作。

Result: √iSWAP门在50 ns内实现99.9%保真度；并行执行两个此类门时保真度降至99.4%；三量子比特门在200 ns内实现95%保真度。

Conclusion: 该中心耦合架构能够显著提升超导量子处理器的连接性，支持跨对角量子比特的高保真度门操作，为大规模量子计算提供了可行的硬件方案。

Abstract: Superconducting quantum processors could significantly profit from enhanced connectivity together with precise control of interactions and gates between qubits. Here we investigate plaquettes of four qubits that are coupled via a central tunable coupling circuit, so that not only gates between qubits connected by an edge of the plaquette can be executed but also between qubits across the diagonal. By numerically and analytically analyzing parametrically driven processes, we explore $\sqrt{\text{iSWAP}}$-gates between any pair of qubits, also across the diagonal, as well as three-qubit interactions and gates. For experimentally available circuit parameters, we for example find $\sqrt{\text{iSWAP}}$-gates with a gate time of 50 ns and 99.9\% fidelity, which is decreased to 99.4\% if two such gates are executed in parallel on disjoint qubit pairs in the plaquette. For three-qubit gates we find fidelities of 95\% fidelity at a gate time of 200 ns.

</details>


### [145] [Dissipation-induced superradiance in matter coupled to a self-interacting cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14526)
*Sebastian Schmid,Matteo Soriente,Oded Zilberberg,Javier del Pino*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究具有光子Kerr非线性的Dicke模型，发现负Kerr非线性可在低阈值下产生自旋反转的超辐射相，且腔耗散能稳定该发光相，为激光和浴工程相开辟新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dicke模型中的超辐射相变阈值通常实验上难以达到，或受到光的抗磁项阻碍，作者希望寻找降低超辐射阈值的新机制。

Method: 在Dicke模型框架下引入光子Kerr非线性（自相互作用），分析正负Kerr非线性对超辐射相变的影响，并考虑腔耗散的作用。

Result: 负Kerr非线性可诱导低阈值超辐射相，伴随自旋反转；该相在封闭系统中不稳定，但腔耗散能使其稳定存在。

Conclusion: 负Kerr非线性为降低超辐射阈值提供新途径，耗散稳定化机制为激光与浴工程相的实现打开了可能性。

Abstract: Light-matter interactions are often modeled via the Dicke model, namely, by two-level systems coupled to a cavity mode. Alas, the threshold for superradiance is often experimentally inaccessible or hindered by light's diamagnetic term. Here, within the Dicke setting, we consider self-interacting light in a cavity, modeled by a photonic Kerr nonlinearity. We show that negative Kerr nonlinearity gives rise to a low-threshold superradiant phase with spin inversion. While unstable in a closed system, cavity dissipation stabilizes this lit phase, opening avenues for lasing and bath-engineered phases.

</details>


### [146] [Scaling native entanglement generation in layered semiconductors with quasi-phase matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14553)
*Benjamin Braun,Andrea Alessandrini,Josip Bajo,Philipp K. Jenke,Leone di Mauro Villari,Birui Yang,Zhi Hao Peng,P. James Schuck,Cory R. Dean,Andrea Marini,Philip Walther,Chiara Trovatello,Lee A. Rozema*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出周期性极化TMD（PPTMD）结构，通过准相位匹配克服相干长度限制，在亚波长厚度下高效产生偏振纠缠光子对，保真度超过99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超薄TMD材料虽然能通过晶体对称性直接产生偏振纠缠光子对，但其转换效率受限于材料相干长度(L_c)，需要一种方法来放大这一相互作用而不破坏对称性产生的纠缠特性。

Method: 通过在TMD材料中以精确的L_c间隔机械翻转非线性系数的符号，引入准相位匹配（QPM），从而在不破坏晶体对称性产生的偏振纠缠前提下，成比例提升光子对产生速率。

Result: PPTMD结构实现了准相位匹配，使光子对产生率显著提升，且偏振纠缠保真度超过99%，验证了该方法在高效产生纠缠光子对方面的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明了薄非线性介质中晶体对称性与传播效应之间的相互作用，为纳米光子系统中量子光源的工程化设计提供了新途径。

Abstract: Efficient generation of entangled photons typically relies on spontaneous parametric down-conversion (SPDC) in phase-matched macroscopic nonlinear media. However, generating entanglement under phase-matching constraints requires additional bulk optics or interferometers. In contrast, ultrathin van der Waals semiconductors - such as transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) - exhibit strong enough optical nonlinearities for SPDC to be observed from subwavelength-thick media, thereby bypassing conventional phase-matching constraints. In this microscopic domain, the intrinsic crystal symmetry governs the nonlinear optical response, enabling the native generation of polarization-entangled photon pairs. However, generating these states efficiently has been fundamentally restricted by the material's coherence length ($L_c$), which limits the attainable conversion efficiency. Here, we investigate periodically-poled TMDs (PPTMDs) designed to scale up this interaction via quasi-phase matching. We demonstrate that mechanically flipping the sign of the nonlinearity at precise intervals of $L_c$ introduces quasi-phase matching, that scales the pair-production rate while preserving the pristine, symmetry-generated polarization entanglement, with fidelities exceeding 99%. Backed by a rigorous theoretical model, our work clarifies the interplay between crystal symmetry and propagation effects in thin nonlinear media, providing a new avenue for engineering quantum light in nanophotonic systems.

</details>


### [147] [On-site interactions in quantum thermal machines: efficiency, rectification and entanglement beyond local and global master equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14593)
*Salvatore Araceli,Teddy H. M. Ong,Baptiste Debecker,Kai Müller,Oliver Lunt,Andrew J. Daley,François Damanet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Redfield主方程和HOPS数值精确方法，研究了连续量子热机中双耦合振子系统的在位相互作用对输运性质的影响，发现相互作用可在高温下调节效率、诱导非互易输运和稳态纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验技术的进步为利用介观量子系统中的非平衡动力学开辟了新途径，但关于弱系统- reservoir耦合下应选择何种约化描述的长期争论尚未解决，且相互作用对量子热输运的影响有待澄清。

Method: 采用Redfield主方程（RME）作为统一框架，插值于局域和全局主方程极限；并借助纯态层级（HOPS）这一数值精确随机方法探索全参数空间。

Result: RME在弱耦合下能准确插值于局域与全局主方程；相互作用可在高温下调节热机效率（低温下基本不变），非对称reservoir耦合可诱导非互易输运，并能在结中产生稳态纠缠。

Conclusion: 在位相互作用可增强量子热力学结的多样性，同时阐明了非马尔可夫性和非线性在量子输运中的作用。

Abstract: Advances in experimental techniques have opened new routes for harnessing non-equilibrium dynamics in mesoscopic quantum systems. In this context, we study the impact of on-site interactions on the transport properties of a continuous quantum thermal machine composed of two coupled oscillators connected to two thermal reservoirs. In the weak system-reservoir coupling regime, where a long-standing debate concerns which reduced description should be preferred, we first show that the Redfield master equation (RME) provides an accurate and unifying framework that interpolates between two well-known limits: the \textit{local} and \textit{global} master equations. By relying on the Hierarchy of Pure States (HOPS), a numerically exact stochastic method, we then explore the full parameter space and show that interactions can be leveraged to tune the efficiency of the thermal machine at high temperatures (while leaving it essentially unchanged at low temperatures), induce non-reciprocal transport under asymmetric reservoir couplings, and generate steady-state entanglement within the junction. We derive expressions for system-bath correlators, such as heat and particle currents, consistently across different frameworks. Our work features on-site interactions to enhance the versatility of quantum thermodynamic junctions and clarifies the role of non-Markovianity and non-linearities in quantum transport.

</details>


### [148] [No classical particle limit for massless quanta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14632)
*Riccardo Falcone,Simon Fuchs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过公理化粗粒化框架证明：在庞加莱群协变性要求下，无质量量子无法在经典极限下产生无质量经典粒子（如经典光子或引力子），但允许场论极限（如电磁场或引力场）的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究相对论性无质量经典粒子是否可能作为无质量量子的经典极限出现，且不依赖特定动力学、环境或指针基。

Method: 发展了纯运动学的公理化粗粒化框架：将候选经典相空间视为POVM的结果空间，仅施加最小经典性条件和时空对称群协变性要求，并应用于庞加莱群。

Result: 证明了无质量粒子的no-go定理：庞加莱群协变性与经典性操作条件不可兼容。

Conclusion: 该定理排除了无质量量子产生经典无质量粒子的可能性（如经典光子、引力子），但保留了场论极限的开放性（如电磁场、引力场的涌现）。

Abstract: We investigate whether relativistic massless classical particles may emerge as the classical limit of massless quanta. To address this question independently of any specific dynamics, environment, or pointer basis, we develop an axiomatic and purely kinematical framework for the coarse-graining approach. In this formulation, a candidate classical phase space is taken as the outcome space of a POVM subject only to minimal classicality and covariance under the relevant spacetime symmetry group. Applying this framework to the Poincaré group, we prove a no-go theorem for massless particles: the covariance requirement is incompatible with the operational conditions for classicality. The theorem leaves open field-like limits of massless quanta, for example the emergence of electromagnetic or gravitational fields, while ruling out classical massless particles, such as classical photons or gravitons.

</details>


### [149] [Quasilinear Equivalence Checking for Detector Error Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14677)
*Mathys Rennela*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为量子纠错中的探测器错误模型（DEM）建立了等式理论及其范畴语义，提出了一个基于对称幺半理论（PROP）和Giry monad的归约系统，证明了每个DEM项存在唯一范式且可在拟线性时间内计算，并给出了首个静态决策过程用于DEM等价性判定，可应用于量子编译器的验证与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DEM在量子编译流水线中广泛用于捕获电路级容错能力，但缺乏严格的形式化等价性理论，无法系统地进行等价判定与优化，亟需一套完整的代数框架来支撑DEM的结构分析与编译器验证。

Method: 1) 建立DEM的等式理论，赋予范畴语义；2) 构造以对称幺半理论（PROP）为基础的项重写系统，并在Giry monad上形式化；3) 证明重写系统是终止、合流且具有唯一范式的；4) 通过Tanner图提供结构不变量，实现拟线性时间O(k|E|log|E|)的范式计算；5) 设计首个静态决策过程，对非自适应量子纠错流水线完备，对部分自适应电路（如晶格手术、分布式QEC）可扩展为可靠且适用的决策过程。

Result: 每个DEM项有唯一范式，可在O(k|E|log|E|)时间内高效计算；首个静态决策过程对非自适应QEC流水线完备，对部分自适应电路可避免指数级开销；提供了完整的结构等价不变量（Tanner图）。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了DEM形式化等价性理论的空白，为量子编译器的验证与优化提供了严格且高效的决策工具，尤其适用于非自适应和部分自适应纠错场景。

Abstract: A Detector Error Model (DEM) is a structured representation of error mechanisms in quantum circuits, which has gained popularity in quantum compilation pipelines for its ability to capture fault-tolerance at a circuit level. It lists error mechanisms as instructions targeting detectors and observables, specifying for each physical fault channel the probability that the fault fires, the detectors it triggers, and the observables it flips.
  In this paper, we develop an equational theory for DEMs, with its associated categorical semantics. We present a sound, terminating, confluent rewriting system for DEM terms, formulating it as a symmetric monoidal theory (a PROP) over the Giry monad. We prove that every DEM term has a unique normal form, which can be computed efficiently in quasilinear time $O(k|E|\log|E|)$, where $|E|$ is the number of instructions and $k$ bounds the size of a target set. This provides a complete set of invariants (via Tanner graphs) for structural DEM equivalence. We provide the first static decision procedure for DEM equivalence, with rigorous correctness guarantees. It is complete (decides full decoder-equivalence exactly) for non-adaptive quantum error correction (QEC) pipelines, and scales to a sound and applicable decision procedure for partially-adaptive circuits (lattice surgery, distributed QEC, ...) without suffering exponential overhead. We discuss its application to the verification and optimisation of quantum compilers.

</details>


### [150] [Resolving the Edge of a Quantum Pyramid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14698)
*Alvan Arulandu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了量子金字塔猜想，即Englert和Řeháček conjectured的等角等概率纯态系综的全局信息最优测量方案。通过证明Holevo和Utkin提出的剩余熵不等式，分别处理了钝角金字塔和扁平金字塔两种情况。对于钝角金字塔，严格证明了局部极小值不可能有三个不同坐标值，并简化为Lambert W函数的代数互反不等式；对于扁平金字塔，证明了零和向量的紧ℓ^p不等式，对所有维度d≥2成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子金字塔猜想，确认等角等概率纯态系综的全局信息最优测量方案。

Method: 对于钝角金字塔，通过证明局部极小值不能有三个不同坐标值，将问题简化为Lambert W函数的代数互反不等式；对于扁平金字塔，使用对称不等式中的等变量方法证明紧ℓ^p不等式。

Result: 证明了剩余的熵不等式，从而确认了量子金字塔猜想；给出了钝角金字塔的严格证明和扁平金字塔在所有维度d≥2上的紧ℓ^p不等式证明。

Conclusion: 成功解决了量子金字塔猜想，其中Lambert W函数的互反不等式和等变量方法可能是独立有趣的贡献。

Abstract: Standing on the shoulders of giants, we resolve the quantum pyramids conjecture, confirming the globally information-optimal measurement for an ensemble of equiangular equiprobable pure states, as conjectured by Englert and Řeháček (arXiv:0905.0510). We do so by proving the remaining entropy inequalities of Holevo and Utkin (arXiv:2506.06700), which certify optimality for obtuse and flat pyramids. For obtuse pyramids, our key contribution is a rigorous proof that local minimizers of the corresponding entropy inequality cannot have three distinct coordinate values. We show that eliminating this family can be reduced to a neat algebraic reciprocal inequality relating branches of the Lambert $W$ function, which may be of independent interest. For flat pyramids, we prove a tight $\ell^p$ inequality for zero-sum vectors that was recently conjectured, proved analytically in dimension $d=3$, and computationally verified for $d\leq 200$ by Holevo and Utkin (arXiv:2603.24017). We prove this bound for all $d\geq 2$ via a technique in symmetric inequalities known as the equal variables method.

</details>
