# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Large Language Model Based Agent for Automated Discovery in Computational Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14266)
*Hang Lin,Chongwen Liu,Gang Yan*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: PhyNex是一个将大语言模型引导搜索与领域特定计算工具相结合的自主体，通过渐进式局部搜索和知识积累，在三个代表性物理问题上自主找到匹配或超越人类专家设计的解决方案，实现最高3.8%（光谱相似性）、15.0%（Max-Cut归一化平均割）和5.9%（量子电池ergotropy）的搜索平均改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算物理中的科学发现常可归结为在物理约束下优化可量化评估的目标，但研究者需投入大量精力迭代优化方法。为加速这一过程，提出了PhyNex自主代理系统。

Method: PhyNex通过耦合大语言模型引导搜索与物理一致性计算工具，采用渐进式局部搜索机制，从成功和失败的尝试中累积可复用知识，生成可解释的探索轨迹。

Result: 在三个任务上：半导体介电光谱预测（光谱相似性提升3.8%）、Max-Cut图割启发式设计（归一化平均割提升15.0%）、Dicke量子电池充电协议优化（ergotropy提升5.9%）。PhyNex自主找到匹配或超越人类科学家的解决方案。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的代理通过结构化的反馈驱动探索，能显著加速从问题规范到有效实现的路径，建议科学家定义目标与约束，自动系统探索方法论搜索空间的实用分工模式。

Abstract: Scientific discovery in computational physics can often be framed as the optimization of quantitatively evaluable objectives subject to physical constraints. While researchers excel at formulating such problems, they frequently devote substantial effort to iterative refinement of methods and solution strategies. To accelerate this process, we introduce PhyNex, an autonomous agent that systematically explores the solution space of scorable scientific tasks by coupling large language model (LLM)-guided search with domain-specific computational tools that enforce physical consistency. PhyNex operates via progressive local search, accumulates reusable knowledge from both successful and failed attempts, and produces interpretable exploration trajectories that reveal which algorithmic components drive performance improvements. We validate PhyNex on three representative and scientifically important problems: predicting frequency-dependent dielectric spectra of semiconductors from crystal structure, designing probabilistic-circuit heuristics for Max-Cut on graphs, and optimizing charging protocols for Dicke quantum batteries in the chaotic coupling regime. Across the three tasks, PhyNex autonomously identifies solutions that match or exceed state-of-the-art approaches designed by human scientists, yielding search-averaged improvements of up to 3.8\% in spectral similarity, up to 15.0\% in normalized mean cut for Max-Cut, and 5.9\% in ergotropy at the $80\mathrm{k}$ training checkpoint in open exploration. These findings demonstrate that LLM-based agents with structured, feedback-driven exploration can substantially accelerate the path from problem specification to effective implementation, suggesting a practical division of labor in which scientists define objectives and constraints while automated systems navigate the methodological search space.

</details>


### [152] [Measuring qualitative change: A variational score for tracking dynamical shifts in partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14430)
*Joseph J. Pollacco,Jonathan Wong,Navonil Neogi,Callum Simpson,Eloisa Bentivegna*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于PDE残差在解变形下行为的准则，用于提高偏微分方程模型搜索的多样性，并开发了实用的计算形式体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏微分方程（PDE）控制着物理和生命科学中众多时空系统的行为，但由于非线性耦合，其解空间难以全面探索。系统化的PDE模型探索方法是计算科学的一个重要目标。

Method: 作者基于PDE残差在解变形下的行为，制定了一个提高搜索多样性的准则，并开发了实用的计算形式体系来实现该准则。

Result: 给出了该准则的计算形式化方法，并在几个感兴趣的案例中展示了其作用。

Conclusion: 所提出的残差变形准则有望为PDE模型空间的系统化探索提供新途径，增加搜索的多样性。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) regulate the behaviour of countless spatiotemporal systems in the physical and life sciences. In many cases, they encode the coupling between the system's degrees of freedom, leading to nonlinear equations whose solution space is challenging to explore exhaustively. Systematic approaches to PDE model exploration are a holy grail of computational science. In this article, we formulate a criterion for increasing the diversity of a search campaign, based on the PDE residual behaviour under solution deformation. We develop a practical formalism to compute this property and illustrate its role in a few cases of interest.

</details>


### [153] [Spin disorder competing with positional symmetry breaking governs the metal-insulator behavior in oxide paramagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14624)
*Jia-Xin Xiong,Xiuwen Zhang,Alex Zunger*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过对称性破缺的密度泛函理论（DFT）研究NaFeO₃和NaOsO₃的顺磁相，揭示了自旋无序与位置对称性破缺之间的竞争是决定过渡金属氧化物顺磁相绝缘或金属行为的关键机制，而不需要强关联效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多过渡金属氧化物在低温下具有反铁磁（AFM）绝缘态，而高温顺磁（PM）相可能绝缘或金属。作者希望在不引入强关联的情况下，解释这种PM相金属-绝缘体行为的差异。

Method: 使用对称性破缺的密度泛函理论（DFT）获得绝缘性NaFeO₃和金属性NaOsO₃的顺磁相，分析自旋无序（导致局域磁矩分布）与位置对称性破缺（导致原子偏移分布）之间的相互作用。

Result: NaFeO₃具有较大的位置位移和较小的自旋无序诱导磁矩分布，导致绝缘性PM相；NaOsO₃则具有显著的自旋无序诱导磁矩分布，迫使PM相变为金属性。

Conclusion: 自旋无序与位置对称性破缺之间的竞争是理解过渡金属氧化物顺磁相金属-绝缘体行为的通用框架，无需引入强关联即可解释看似迥异的电导行为。

Abstract: Numerous transition-metal oxides have low-temperature antiferromagnetic (AFM) states and high-temperature paramagnetic (PM) phases, where the AFM state is usually insulating while the PM phase can be either insulating or metallic. Without involving strong correlation, we use symmetry-broken density-functional theory (DFT) to obtain the PM phases of insulating NaFeO3 vs the recently discovered metallic NaOsO3. We develop the understanding of insulating and metallic behaviors in paramagnetic oxides by analyzing the interactions between magnetic and positional symmetry breaking: The insulating gap is governed by the competition between the spin disorder that induces a distribution of different magnitudes of local magnetic moments and the polymorphous distribution of off-center atomic displacements. NaFeO3, on the other hand, has large positional displacement with small spin-disorder-induced moments distribution, leading to insulating PM phase, whereas NaOsO3 has a pronounced spin-disorder-induced moments distribution that forces the PM phase to become metallic. Our work identifies this symmetry-breaking competition as a general framework to bridge seemingly disparate metal-insulator behaviors in transition-metal oxides paramagnets without invoking strong correlation.

</details>
